# 2단계: 직원용 웹(React) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 직원(접수직원/의사/관리자)이 사용하는 React 웹 앱을 만들고, 2단계 스펙에서 새로 필요해진 백엔드 3종(설정값 확장, 진료문구, 일정변경/재예약)을 1단계 FastAPI 백엔드에 추가한다.

**Architecture:** 백엔드는 1단계에서 만든 `acquire_as`/`require_role`/`AppError` 패턴을 그대로 재사용해 신규 서비스·라우터를 추가한다. 프론트엔드는 Vite+React+TypeScript SPA로, Supabase Auth로 로그인하고 역할에 따라 라우팅을 분기하며, React Query로 백엔드 REST API를 호출하고 일부 화면은 Supabase Realtime을 구독해 동기화한다.

**Tech Stack:** React, TypeScript, Vite, React Router, TanStack React Query, `@supabase/supabase-js`, FullCalendar(`@fullcalendar/react`), `@dnd-kit/core`(드래그앤드롭), Vitest + Testing Library + MSW(테스트)

## Global Constraints

- **[정합성 검토 P1/P2 추적]** `docs/supabase-postgres-review-2026-07-28.md`의 SDB-07~SDB-16, SDB-24~SDB-34는 P0가 아니라 해당 기능(예: 일정 규칙·슬롯 재생성 동시성 SDB-26, 목록 페이지네이션 SDB-32, 슬롯 재생성 N+1 SDB-33)을 실제로 만들 때 함께 반영하기로 결정됨(2026-07-28).
- 마이그레이션 번호 대역: **2단계(직원 웹) `00100~00199`**(1단계 `00001~00099`와 겹치지 않도록 미리 고정된 대역, 정합성 검토 SDB-01). 신규 마이그레이션은 `supabase/migrations/00100`부터 번호를 이어간다
- 백엔드 신규 코드는 1단계에서 만든 `backend/app/db/pool.py`(`acquire_as`), `backend/app/core/security.py`(`StaffContext`, `require_role`), `backend/app/core/errors.py`(`AppError`)를 그대로 재사용한다 — 새로 만들지 않는다
- 프론트엔드는 `frontend/` 디렉토리에 둔다(백엔드는 `backend/`)
- 화면 스타일(CSS 디자인)은 이 계획의 범위 밖이다 — 시맨틱 HTML과 최소한의 클래스명만 사용하고, 정교한 비주얼 디자인은 다루지 않는다
- 모든 API 오류 메시지는 한글로 노출한다(1단계 `AppError` 패턴 그대로)
- 여러 직원이 동시에 보는 화면(오늘의 현황/캘린더/대기목록)은 Supabase Realtime으로 동기화한다
- 동명이인 재확인 팝업은 접수직원의 환자 선택·예약확정 시점에만 적용하고, 의사 화면에는 적용하지 않는다
- 대기 순서 변경은 드래그앤드롭이며, 드롭 시 사유 입력 모달이 뜨고 취소하면 원래 위치로 되돌아간다

## Task 1: 마이그레이션 — hospital_settings 확장 + doctor_quick_phrases

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/00100_staff_web_additions.sql`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_staff_web_additions_schema.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `tests.conftest.db_conn`, `seed_staff`, `set_session_auth`(1단계 Task 2), `staff`, `hospital_settings`(1단계 Task 8)
- Produces: `hospital_settings.long_wait_threshold_minutes` 컬럼(기본 30), 테이블 `doctor_quick_phrases(id, doctor_id, text, created_at)`

- [ ] **Step 1: 마이그레이션 SQL 작성**

`supabase/migrations/00100_staff_web_additions.sql`:
```sql
alter table hospital_settings
  add column long_wait_threshold_minutes int not null default 30;

create table doctor_quick_phrases (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  doctor_id uuid not null references staff(id),
  text text not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table doctor_quick_phrases enable row level security;

create policy "staff_can_read_quick_phrases" on doctor_quick_phrases
  for select
  using (private.is_active_staff());

create policy "doctor_can_manage_own_quick_phrases" on doctor_quick_phrases
  for all
  using (private.current_staff_role() = 'doctor' and private.current_staff_id() = doctor_quick_phrases.doctor_id)
  with check (private.current_staff_role() = 'doctor' and private.current_staff_id() = doctor_quick_phrases.doctor_id);
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 마이그레이션 적용**

Run: `supabase db reset`
Expected: 오류 없이 적용됨

```python
import pytest
from tests.conftest import seed_staff, set_session_auth


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_hospital_settings_has_wait_threshold(db_conn):
    value = await db_conn.fetchval("select long_wait_threshold_minutes from hospital_settings")
    assert value == 30


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_doctor_can_create_own_quick_phrase(db_conn):
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    await set_session_auth(db_conn, doctor["auth_user_id"])

    phrase_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into doctor_quick_phrases (doctor_id, text) values ($1, '충분한 휴식과 수분 섭취를 권장합니다') returning id",
        doctor["staff_id"],
    )
    assert phrase_id is not None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_doctor_cannot_create_phrase_for_other_doctor(db_conn):
    doctor_a = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    doctor_b = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    await set_session_auth(db_conn, doctor_a["auth_user_id"])

    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        await db_conn.execute(
            "insert into doctor_quick_phrases (doctor_id, text) values ($1, '문구')",
            doctor_b["staff_id"],
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_receptionist_can_read_but_not_write_phrases(db_conn):
    admin = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin")
    receptionist = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    await set_session_auth(db_conn, admin["auth_user_id"])
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into doctor_quick_phrases (doctor_id, text) values ($1, '문구')", doctor["staff_id"],
    )

    await set_session_auth(db_conn, receptionist["auth_user_id"])
    rows = await db_conn.fetch("select * from doctor_quick_phrases")
    assert len(rows) == 1

    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        await db_conn.execute(
            "insert into doctor_quick_phrases (doctor_id, text) values ($1, '새문구')", doctor["staff_id"],
        )
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/services/appointment_service.py`
- Create: `backend/app/services/schedule_service.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_appointment_service.py` (추가)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_schedule_service.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.db.pool.acquire_as`, `app.core.errors.AppError`, `app.core.security.StaffContext`(1단계 Task 9), `app.services.slot_service.book_slot`(1단계 Task 13)
- Produces: `app.services.appointment_service.reschedule_appointment(appointment_id, new_slot_id, staff, reason) -> None`
- Produces: `app.services.schedule_service.list_affected_appointments(doctor_id, staff) -> list[dict]` (각 dict: `id, reason, patient_name, masked_phone, slot_date, start_time` — [정합성 검토 R5-06] `patient_phone`(원본)을 `masked_phone`으로 교체)
- Produces([정합성 검토 R5-06]): `app.core.masking.mask_phone(phone: str) -> str`(중간 4자리 마스킹, `010-****-1234`), `mask_birth_date(birth_date: date) -> str`(월만 마스킹, `1990-**-15`) — 목록류 API 전체가 공용으로 재사용하는 유틸. 서버가 마스킹된 값만 응답 DTO에 담는다(클라이언트가 원본을 받아 화면에서만 가리는 방식은 쓰지 않는다).

```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_reschedule_appointment_moves_slot_and_frees_old_one(db_conn):
    ctx = await _seed_base(db_conn)
    old_slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time) values ($1, '2026-08-01', '09:00') returning id",
        ctx["doctor"].id,
    )
    new_slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time) values ($1, '2026-08-03', '10:00') returning id",
        ctx["doctor"].id,
    )
    appointment_id = await appointment_service.create_appointment(
        staff=ctx["receptionist"],
        account_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"],
        for_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"],
        department_id=ctx["dept_id"],
        doctor_id=ctx["doctor"].id,
        reason="감기",
        source="staff",
        initial_status="예약확정",
        slot_id=old_slot_id,
    )

    await appointment_service.reschedule_appointment(
        appointment_id, new_slot_id, ctx["receptionist"], reason="의사 일정 변경으로 재조정",
    )

    row = await db_conn.fetchrow("select slot_id, status from appointments where id = $1", appointment_id)
    assert row["slot_id"] == new_slot_id
    assert row["status"] == "예약확정"

    old_slot_status = await db_conn.fetchval("select status from appointment_slots where id = $1", old_slot_id)
    assert old_slot_status == "빈시간"

    new_slot_status = await db_conn.fetchval("select status from appointment_slots where id = $1", new_slot_id)
    assert new_slot_status == "예약됨"

    assert old_slot_status == "휴진"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: reschedule_appointment 구현**

`backend/app/services/appointment_service.py`에 추가:
```python
async def reschedule_appointment(
    appointment_id: UUID,
    new_slot_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext,
    reason: str,
) -> None:
    if not reason.strip():
        raise AppError("일정변경 사유를 입력해야 합니다.", status_code=400)

    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select slot_id, status from appointments where id = $1", appointment_id,
        )
        if row is None:
            raise AppError("예약을 찾을 수 없습니다.", status_code=404)

        new_slot = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select doctor_id from appointment_slots where id = $1", new_slot_id,
        )
        if new_slot is None:
            raise AppError("새 시간을 찾을 수 없습니다.", status_code=404)

        booked = await book_slot(new_slot_id, staff, conn=conn)
        if not booked:
            raise AppError("이미 예약된 시간입니다. 다른 시간을 선택하세요.", status_code=409)

        old_slot_id = row["slot_id"]
        if old_slot_id is not None:
            old_slot = await conn.fetchrow(
                "select doctor_id, slot_date from appointment_slots where id = $1", old_slot_id,
            )
            is_closed = await conn.fetchval(
                "select is_closed from doctor_schedule_exceptions where doctor_id = $1 and exception_date = $2",
                old_slot["doctor_id"], old_slot["slot_date"],
            )
            freed_status = "휴진" if is_closed else "빈시간"
            await conn.execute(
                "update appointment_slots set status = $1 where id = $2", freed_status, old_slot_id,
            )

        await conn.execute(
            "update appointments set slot_id = $1, doctor_id = $2, updated_at = now() where id = $3",
            new_slot_id, new_slot["doctor_id"], appointment_id,
        )
        await conn.execute(
            """
            insert into appointment_status_history (appointment_id, from_status, to_status, changed_by, reason)
            values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4)
            """,
            appointment_id, row["status"], staff.id, reason,
        )
```

`backend/tests/test_schedule_service.py`:
```python
import pytest

from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.services import schedule_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str) -> StaffContext:
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=None)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_affected_appointments_finds_closed_day_conflicts(db_conn):
    admin_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin")
    admin = _to_context(admin_seed, "admin")
    receptionist_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    doctor_id = doctor_seed["staff_id"]

    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time, status) values ($1, '2026-08-01', '09:00', '예약됨') returning id",
        doctor_id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into appointments
            (slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4, '예약확정', 'staff', $5)
        """,
        slot_id, patient_id, dept_id, doctor_id, receptionist_seed["staff_id"],
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into doctor_schedule_exceptions (doctor_id, exception_date, is_closed) values ($1, '2026-08-01', true)",
        doctor_id,
    )

    affected = await schedule_service.list_affected_appointments(doctor_id, admin)

    assert len(affected) == 1
    assert affected[0]["patient_name"] == "홍길동"
    assert affected[0]["masked_phone"].count("*") == 4  # [정합성 검토 R5-06] 중간 4자리 마스킹
    assert "patient_phone" not in affected[0]  # 원본 phone 필드는 응답에 없다


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_affected_appointments_excludes_cancelled(db_conn):
    admin_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin")
    admin = _to_context(admin_seed, "admin")
    receptionist_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    doctor_id = doctor_seed["staff_id"]

    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time, status) values ($1, '2026-08-01', '09:00', '예약됨') returning id",
        doctor_id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into appointments
            (slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4, '환자취소', 'staff', $5)
        """,
        slot_id, patient_id, dept_id, doctor_id, receptionist_seed["staff_id"],
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into doctor_schedule_exceptions (doctor_id, exception_date, is_closed) values ($1, '2026-08-01', true)",
        doctor_id,
    )

    affected = await schedule_service.list_affected_appointments(doctor_id, admin)

    assert affected == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_affected_appointments_previews_unsaved_candidate_exception(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-02] exception_date를 후보값으로 넘기면, doctor_schedule_exceptions에
    아직 아무 행도 저장돼 있지 않아도 영향받는 예약을 정확히 찾아야 한다(저장 전 미리보기)."""
    from datetime import date

    admin_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin")
    admin = _to_context(admin_seed, "admin")
    receptionist_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor_seed = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    doctor_id = doctor_seed["staff_id"]

    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time, status) values ($1, '2026-08-01', '09:00', '예약됨') returning id",
        doctor_id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into appointments
            (slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4, '예약확정', 'staff', $5)
        """,
        slot_id, patient_id, dept_id, doctor_id, receptionist_seed["staff_id"],
    )
    # doctor_schedule_exceptions에는 아무 행도 저장하지 않는다 — 관리자가 화면에 날짜만 입력한 상태를 재현.

    affected = await schedule_service.list_affected_appointments(
        doctor_id, admin, exception_date="2026-08-01", is_closed=True,
    )

    assert len(affected) == 1
    assert affected[0]["slot_date"] == date(2026, 8, 1)

    # 후보 날짜와 다른 날에는 영향이 없어야 한다.
    unaffected = await schedule_service.list_affected_appointments(
        doctor_id, admin, exception_date="2026-08-02", is_closed=True,
    )
    assert unaffected == []
```

Expected: FAIL (`app.services.schedule_service` 모듈 없음)

- [ ] **Step 4.5: [정합성 검토 R5-06] 공용 마스킹 유틸 작성**

`backend/app/core/masking.py`:
```python
from datetime import date


def mask_phone(phone: str) -> str:
    """중간 4자리를 마스킹한다: 01012345678 -> 010-****-1234. 형식이 예상과 다르면 안전하게 그대로 반환하지 않고
    끝 4자리만 남기고 나머지를 별표로 채운다(원본을 실수로 그대로 내보내는 사고를 막기 위함)."""
    digits = "".join(ch for ch in phone if ch.isdigit())
    if len(digits) < 8:
        return "*" * len(digits)
    return f"{digits[:3]}-****-{digits[-4:]}"


def mask_birth_date(birth_date: date) -> str:
    """월만 마스킹한다: 1990-03-15 -> 1990-**-15."""
    return f"{birth_date.year:04d}-**-{birth_date.day:02d}"
```

- [ ] **Step 5: schedule_service 구현**

`backend/app/services/schedule_service.py`:
```python
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.masking import mask_phone  # [정합성 검토 R5-06]
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as

ACTIVE_STATUSES = ["예약신청", "예약확정", "도착", "진료대기", "진료중"]


async def list_affected_appointments(
    doctor_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext,
    exception_date: str | None = None,
    is_closed: bool | None = None,
    override_start_time: str | None = None,
    override_end_time: str | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    """[정합성 검토 R3-02] exception_date가 주어지면(관리자가 아직 저장 버튼을 누르기 전, 화면에
    입력만 해둔 후보 예외값) 그 값으로 직접 영향받는 예약을 계산하는 저장 전 미리보기(dry-run)로
    동작한다. 이전 버전은 이 함수가 항상 `doctor_schedule_exceptions`에 이미 저장된 행만 조회했기
    때문에, 관리자가 새 휴진일을 입력한 시점에는 그 행이 아직 없어 다이얼로그가 실제로는 아무 영향도
    없는 것처럼(또는 엉뚱한 과거 예외 기준으로) 보여줬다. exception_date가 없으면(하위 호환) 기존처럼
    이미 저장된 예외 기준으로 조회한다."""
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        if exception_date is not None:
            rows = await conn.fetch(
                """
                select a.id, a.reason, p.name as patient_name, p.phone as raw_phone,
                       s.slot_date, s.start_time
                from appointments a
                join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
                join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
                where a.doctor_id = $1
                  and a.status = any($2::text[])
                  and s.slot_date = $3
                  and (
                        $4
                        or s.start_time < $5
                        or s.start_time >= $6
                  )
                order by s.slot_date, s.start_time
                """,
                doctor_id, ACTIVE_STATUSES, exception_date, bool(is_closed), override_start_time, override_end_time,
            )
        else:
            rows = await conn.fetch(
                """
                select a.id, a.reason, p.name as patient_name, p.phone as raw_phone,
                       s.slot_date, s.start_time
                from appointments a
                join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
                join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
                join doctor_schedule_exceptions e
                    on e.doctor_id = a.doctor_id and e.exception_date = s.slot_date
                where a.doctor_id = $1
                  and a.status = any($2::text[])
                  and (
                        e.is_closed
                        or s.start_time < e.override_start_time
                        or s.start_time >= e.override_end_time
                  )
                order by s.slot_date, s.start_time
                """,
                doctor_id, ACTIVE_STATUSES,
            )
    # [정합성 검토 R5-06] 원본 phone은 dict에 담지 않는다 — masked_phone만 응답에 포함한다.
    return [
        {
            "id": row["id"], "reason": row["reason"], "patient_name": row["patient_name"],
            "masked_phone": mask_phone(row["raw_phone"]),
            "slot_date": row["slot_date"], "start_time": row["start_time"],
        }
        for row in rows
    ]
```

```python
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as


async def list_phrases(doctor_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            "select id, text from doctor_quick_phrases where doctor_id = $1 order by created_at", doctor_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def create_phrase(doctor_id: UUID, text: str, staff: StaffContext) -> UUID:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        return await conn.fetchval(
            "insert into doctor_quick_phrases (doctor_id, text) values ($1, $2) returning id",
            doctor_id, text,
        )
```

```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.core.security import StaffContext, get_current_staff

router = APIRouter(tags=["me"])


class MeResponse(BaseModel):
    staff_id: UUID
    role: str
    department_id: UUID | None


@router.get("/me", response_model=MeResponse)
async def read_me(staff: StaffContext = Depends(get_current_staff)) -> MeResponse:
    return MeResponse(staff_id=staff.id, role=staff.role, department_id=staff.department_id)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: quick_phrases 라우터 작성**

`backend/app/routers/quick_phrases.py`:
```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import quick_phrase_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/quick-phrases", tags=["quick-phrases"])


class PhraseResponse(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    text: str


@router.get("", response_model=list[PhraseResponse])
async def list_phrases(
    doctor_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> list[PhraseResponse]:
    phrases = await quick_phrase_service.list_phrases(doctor_id, staff)
    return [PhraseResponse(**p) for p in phrases]


class CreatePhraseRequest(BaseModel):
    doctor_id: UUID
    text: str


class CreatePhraseResponse(BaseModel):
    id: UUID


@router.post("", response_model=CreatePhraseResponse)
async def create_phrase(
    body: CreatePhraseRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("doctor")),
) -> CreatePhraseResponse:
    phrase_id = await quick_phrase_service.create_phrase(body.doctor_id, body.text, staff)
    return CreatePhraseResponse(id=phrase_id)
```

- [ ] **Step 6: appointments 라우터에 reschedule/affected 엔드포인트 추가**

`backend/app/routers/appointments.py`에 추가:
```python
from app.services import schedule_service


class RescheduleRequest(BaseModel):
    new_slot_id: UUID
    reason: str


@router.post("/{appointment_id}/reschedule")
async def reschedule(
    appointment_id: UUID,
    body: RescheduleRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> dict:
    await appointment_service.reschedule_appointment(appointment_id, body.new_slot_id, staff, body.reason)
    return {"status": "rescheduled"}


class AffectedAppointmentResponse(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    reason: str | None
    patient_name: str
    masked_phone: str  # [정합성 검토 R5-06]
    slot_date: str
    start_time: str


@router.get("/affected", response_model=list[AffectedAppointmentResponse])
async def affected_appointments(
    doctor_id: UUID,
    exception_date: str | None = None,
    is_closed: bool | None = None,
    override_start_time: str | None = None,
    override_end_time: str | None = None,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> list[AffectedAppointmentResponse]:
    # [정합성 검토 R3-02] exception_date가 있으면 저장 전 후보값으로 미리보기(dry-run)한다.
    rows = await schedule_service.list_affected_appointments(
        doctor_id, staff,
        exception_date=exception_date, is_closed=is_closed,
        override_start_time=override_start_time, override_end_time=override_end_time,
    )
    return [
        AffectedAppointmentResponse(
            id=row["id"],
            reason=row["reason"],
            patient_name=row["patient_name"],
            masked_phone=row["masked_phone"],
            slot_date=str(row["slot_date"]),
            start_time=str(row["start_time"]),
        )
        for row in rows
    ]
```

```tsx
import { RequireRole } from "./RequireRole";

vi.mock("../lib/supabaseClient", () => ({

    auth: {
      getSession: async () => ({
        data: { session: { access_token: "fake-token" } },
      }),
      onAuthStateChange: () => ({ data: { subscription: { unsubscribe: () => {} } } }),
    },
  },
}));

describe("RequireRole", () => {
  it("renders children when role matches", async () => {
    render(
      <AuthProvider apiBaseUrl="http://localhost:8000">
        <RequireRole roles={["admin"]}>
          <div>관리자 화면</div>
        </RequireRole>
      </AuthProvider>,
    );

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("관리자 화면")).toBeInTheDocument());

      <AuthProvider apiBaseUrl="http://localhost:8000">

      </AuthProvider>,
    );

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("이 화면에 접근할 권한이 없습니다.")).toBeInTheDocument());
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { createContext, type ReactNode, useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { supabase } from "../lib/supabaseClient";

export type StaffInfo = {
  staffId: string;
  role: "receptionist" | "doctor" | "admin";
  departmentId: string | null;
};

type AuthContextValue = {
  staff: StaffInfo | null;
  loading: boolean;
};

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue>({ staff: null, loading: true });

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
  apiBaseUrl,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  apiBaseUrl: string;
}) {
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState<StaffInfo | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadStaff() {
      const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      const token = data.session?.access_token;
      if (!token) {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/me`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      });
      if (response.ok) {
        const body = await response.json();
        if (!cancelled) {
          setStaff({ staffId: body.staff_id, role: body.role, departmentId: body.department_id });
        }
      }
      if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
    }

    loadStaff();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [apiBaseUrl]);

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={{ staff, loading }}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/useAuth.ts`:
```typescript

import { AuthContext } from "./AuthProvider";

export function useAuth() {
  return useContext(AuthContext);
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/RequireRole.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { useAuth } from "./useAuth";

export function RequireRole({ roles, children }: { roles: string[]; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { staff, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;
  if (!staff || !roles.includes(staff.role)) {
    return <p>이 화면에 접근할 권한이 없습니다.</p>;
  }
  return <>{children}</>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: httpClient 구현**

`frontend/src/api/httpClient.ts`:
```typescript
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabaseClient";

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL as string;

export class ApiError extends Error {}

export async function apiFetch<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
  const token = data.session?.access_token;

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...options.headers,
    },
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const body = await response.json().catch(() => ({ detail: "요청 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다." }));
    throw new ApiError(body.detail ?? "요청 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
  }

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return undefined as T;
  }
  return (await response.json()) as T;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: appointments API 구현**

`frontend/src/api/appointments.ts`:
```typescript
import { apiFetch } from "./httpClient";

export type CreateAppointmentBody = {
  account_patient_id: string;
  for_patient_id: string;
  department_id: string;
  doctor_id: string;
  reason: string;
  source: "app" | "chatbot" | "staff";
  initial_status: string;
  slot_id?: string;
};

export function createAppointment(body: CreateAppointmentBody) {
  return apiFetch<{ appointment_id: string }>("/appointments", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

export function transitionStatus(
  appointmentId: string,
  body: { new_status: string; reason: string | null; expected_updated_at: string },
) {
  return apiFetch<void>(`/appointments/${appointmentId}/status`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

export function reorderQueue(appointmentId: string, body: { new_position: number; reason: string }) {
  return apiFetch<void>(`/appointments/${appointmentId}/queue-position`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

export function setUrgentFlag(
  appointmentId: string,
  body: { is_urgent: boolean; expected_updated_at: string },
) {
  return apiFetch<void>(`/appointments/${appointmentId}/urgent-flag`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

export function rescheduleAppointment(
  appointmentId: string,
  body: { new_slot_id: string; reason: string },
) {
  return apiFetch<void>(`/appointments/${appointmentId}/reschedule`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

export type AffectedAppointment = {
  id: string;
  reason: string | null;
  patient_name: string;
  masked_phone: string; // [정합성 검토 R5-06]
  slot_date: string;
  start_time: string;
};

export type CandidateException = {
  exceptionDate: string;
  isClosed: boolean;
  overrideStartTime?: string;
  overrideEndTime?: string;
};

export function listAffectedAppointments(doctorId: string, candidate?: CandidateException) {
  // [정합성 검토 R3-02] candidate가 있으면 아직 저장하지 않은 후보 예외값으로 미리보기 조회한다.
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ doctor_id: doctorId });
  if (candidate) {
    params.set("exception_date", candidate.exceptionDate);
    params.set("is_closed", String(candidate.isClosed));
    if (candidate.overrideStartTime) params.set("override_start_time", candidate.overrideStartTime);
    if (candidate.overrideEndTime) params.set("override_end_time", candidate.overrideEndTime);
  }
  return apiFetch<AffectedAppointment[]>(`/appointments/affected?${params}`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: patients 라우터 구현**

`backend/app/routers/patients.py`:
```python
from datetime import date
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import patient_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/patients", tags=["patients"])


class RegisterPatientRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    birth_date: date
    gender: str
    phone: str


class RegisterPatientResponse(BaseModel):
    patient_id: UUID


@router.post("", response_model=RegisterPatientResponse)
async def register_patient(
    body: RegisterPatientRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> RegisterPatientResponse:
    patient_id = await patient_service.register_patient(
        body.name, body.birth_date, body.gender, body.phone, staff,
    )
    return RegisterPatientResponse(patient_id=patient_id)


class FindPatientResponse(BaseModel):
    patient_id: UUID | None


@router.get("/find", response_model=FindPatientResponse)
async def find_patient(
    phone: str,
    birth_date: date,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> FindPatientResponse:
    patient_id = await patient_service.find_by_phone_and_birthdate(phone, birth_date, staff)
    return FindPatientResponse(patient_id=patient_id)
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { ReasonPromptDialog } from "./ReasonPromptDialog";

describe("ReasonPromptDialog", () => {
  it("disables confirm button until reason is entered", async () => {
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();
    render(<ReasonPromptDialog title="대기 순서 변경" onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={() => {}} />);

    const confirmButton = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "변경 확인" });
    expect(confirmButton).toBeDisabled();

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("변경 사유"), "응급환자 우선");
    expect(confirmButton).toBeEnabled();

    await userEvent.click(confirmButton);
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalledWith("응급환자 우선");
  });

  it("calls onCancel without calling onConfirm", async () => {
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();
    const onCancel = vi.fn();
    render(<ReasonPromptDialog title="대기 순서 변경" onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={onCancel} />);

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "취소" }));
    expect(onCancel).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(onConfirm).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: ReasonPromptDialog 구현**

`frontend/src/components/ReasonPromptDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  title: string;
  onConfirm: (reason: string) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

export function ReasonPromptDialog({ title, onConfirm, onCancel }: Props) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");

  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label={title}>
      <p>{title}</p>
      <label htmlFor="reason-input">변경 사유</label>
      <input id="reason-input" value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} />
      <button type="button" onClick={onCancel}>
        취소
      </button>
      <button type="button" disabled={reason.trim() === ""} onClick={() => onConfirm(reason)}>
        변경 확인
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
type Props = {
  label: string;
  value: number | string;
  onClick?: () => void;
};

export function StatTile({ label, value, onClick }: Props) {
  const Tag = onClick ? "button" : "div";
  return (
    <Tag className="stat-tile" onClick={onClick} type={onClick ? "button" : undefined}>
      <p className="stat-tile-label">{label}</p>
      <p className="stat-tile-value">{value}</p>
    </Tag>
  );
}
```

```typescript
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

export type TodaySummary = {
  long_wait_patients: { appointment_id: string; patient_name: string }[];
  urgent_patients: { appointment_id: string; patient_name: string }[];
  pending_inquiries_count: number;
  affected_appointments_count: number;
  total_reserved: number;
  arrived: number;
  waiting: number;
  in_progress: number;
  completed: number;
  cancelled_or_no_show: number;
};

export function useTodaySummary() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["today-summary"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<TodaySummary>("/today-summary"),
  });
}
```

`frontend/src/features/today/TodayPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { StatTile } from "../../components/StatTile";
import { useTodaySummary } from "./useTodaySummary";

export function TodayPage() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useTodaySummary();

  if (isLoading || !data) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  const hasActionItems =
    data.long_wait_patients.length > 0 ||
    data.urgent_patients.length > 0 ||
    data.pending_inquiries_count > 0 ||
    data.affected_appointments_count > 0;

  return (
    <div>
      {hasActionItems && (
        <section aria-label="지금 처리할 것">
          {data.long_wait_patients.map((p) => (
            <p key={p.appointment_id}>오래 대기: {p.patient_name}</p>
          ))}
          {data.urgent_patients.map((p) => (
            <p key={p.appointment_id}>응급/주의: {p.patient_name}</p>
          ))}
          {data.pending_inquiries_count > 0 && <p>확인 필요 상담 문의: {data.pending_inquiries_count}건</p>}
          {data.affected_appointments_count > 0 && (
            <p>일정변경으로 확인 필요한 예약: {data.affected_appointments_count}건</p>
          )}
        </section>
      )}
      <section aria-label="오늘 요약">
        <StatTile label="전체 예약" value={data.total_reserved} />
        <StatTile label="도착" value={data.arrived} />
        <StatTile label="대기" value={data.waiting} />
        <StatTile label="진료중" value={data.in_progress} />
        <StatTile label="완료" value={data.completed} />
        <StatTile label="취소/부도" value={data.cancelled_or_no_show} />
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

주: `/today-summary` 백엔드 엔드포인트는 여러 테이블(appointments, hospital_settings)을 집계하는 새 조회 전용 엔드포인트로, 이 태스크에서는 프론트엔드만 구현하고 백엔드는 Task 9(대기 목록)와 함께 처리하지 않고 별도로 필요 — 아래 Task 13에서 통계·요약 엔드포인트를 한 번에 다룬다.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `reorderQueue, setUrgentFlag`(Task 5), `<ReasonPromptDialog>`(Task 7), `findPatient, registerPatient`(Task 5), `<ConfirmIdentityDialog>`(Task 7), `createAppointment`(Task 5)
- Produces: `<QueueRow appointment onReorderConfirmed onUrgentToggled />` — 드래그앤드롭은 상위 `QueuePage`에서 `@dnd-kit/core`로 처리하고, `QueueRow`는 드롭 이후 뜨는 사유 모달과 확정/취소 로직을 담당

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";

import { QueueRow } from "./QueueRow";

const appointment = {
  id: "a1",
  patientName: "홍길동",
  patientBirthDate: "1985-03-01",
  waitMinutes: 12,
  departmentName: "내과",

  updatedAt: "2026-07-27T00:00:00Z",
};

describe("QueueRow", () => {
  it("shows a reason prompt after a reorder is proposed, and confirms with the reason", async () => {
    const onReorderConfirmed = vi.fn();
    render(
      <QueueRow
        appointment={appointment}
        pendingNewPosition={3}
        onReorderConfirmed={onReorderConfirmed}
        onReorderCancelled={() => {}}
        onUrgentToggled={vi.fn()}
      />,
    );

    expect(screen.getByText(/1번 → 3번/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("변경 사유"), "응급환자 우선");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "변경 확인" }));

    expect(onReorderConfirmed).toHaveBeenCalledWith("a1", 3, "응급환자 우선");
  });

  it("shows a confirmation before toggling the urgent flag", async () => {
    const onUrgentToggled = vi.fn();
    render(
      <QueueRow
        appointment={appointment}
        pendingNewPosition={null}
        onReorderConfirmed={vi.fn()}

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "응급/주의 표시" }));

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));
    expect(onUrgentToggled).toHaveBeenCalledWith("a1", true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: QueueRow 구현**

`frontend/src/features/queue/QueueRow.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";

import { ReasonPromptDialog } from "../../components/ReasonPromptDialog";

export type QueueAppointment = {
  id: string;
  patientName: string;
  patientBirthDate: string;
  waitMinutes: number;
  departmentName: string;
  doctorName: string;
  isUrgent: boolean;
  updatedAt: string;
};

type Props = {
  appointment: QueueAppointment;
  currentPosition?: number;
  pendingNewPosition: number | null;
  onReorderConfirmed: (appointmentId: string, newPosition: number, reason: string) => void;
  onReorderCancelled: () => void;
  onUrgentToggled: (appointmentId: string, isUrgent: boolean) => void;
};

export function QueueRow({
  appointment,
  currentPosition = 1,
  pendingNewPosition,
  onReorderConfirmed,
  onReorderCancelled,
  onUrgentToggled,
}: Props) {
  const [showUrgentConfirm, setShowUrgentConfirm] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div role="listitem">
      <span>{appointment.patientName}</span>
      <span>({appointment.patientBirthDate})</span>
      <span>{appointment.waitMinutes}분 대기</span>
      <span>
        {appointment.departmentName}/{appointment.doctorName}
      </span>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowUrgentConfirm(true)}>
        응급/주의 표시
      </button>

      {showUrgentConfirm && (
        <div role="dialog">
          <p>의학적 응급도 판정이 아닙니다. 응급 표시를 하시겠습니까?</p>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowUrgentConfirm(false)}>
            취소
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setShowUrgentConfirm(false);
              onUrgentToggled(appointment.id, !appointment.isUrgent);
            }}
          >
            확인
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {pendingNewPosition !== null && (
        <div>
          <p>
            {appointment.patientName} 님의 대기 순서를 {currentPosition}번 → {pendingNewPosition}번으로 변경합니다
          </p>
          <ReasonPromptDialog
            title="대기 순서 변경"
            onCancel={onReorderCancelled}
            onConfirm={(reason) => onReorderConfirmed(appointment.id, pendingNewPosition, reason)}
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: QueuePage 구현 (드래그앤드롭 컨테이너, 테스트 없이 QueueRow 조합)**

`frontend/src/features/queue/QueuePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { DndContext, type DragEndEvent } from "@dnd-kit/core";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";
import { reorderQueue, setUrgentFlag } from "../../api/appointments";
import { QueueAppointment, QueueRow } from "./QueueRow";

export function QueuePage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: appointments } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["queue"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<QueueAppointment[]>("/queue-today"),
  });
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ id: string; newPosition: number } | null>(null);

  function handleDragEnd(event: DragEndEvent) {
    if (!event.over || event.active.id === event.over.id || !appointments) return;
    const newIndex = appointments.findIndex((a) => a.id === event.over!.id);
    setPending({ id: String(event.active.id), newPosition: newIndex + 1 });
  }

  async function handleReorderConfirmed(appointmentId: string, newPosition: number, reason: string) {
    await reorderQueue(appointmentId, { new_position: newPosition, reason });
    setPending(null);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["queue"] });
  }

  async function handleUrgentToggled(appointmentId: string, isUrgent: boolean) {
    const target = appointments?.find((a) => a.id === appointmentId);
    if (!target) return;
    await setUrgentFlag(appointmentId, { is_urgent: isUrgent, expected_updated_at: target.updatedAt });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["queue"] });
  }

  if (!appointments) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  return (
    <DndContext onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}>
      <div role="list">
        {appointments.map((appointment, index) => (
          <QueueRow
            key={appointment.id}
            appointment={appointment}
            currentPosition={index + 1}
            pendingNewPosition={pending?.id === appointment.id ? pending.newPosition : null}
            onReorderConfirmed={handleReorderConfirmed}
            onReorderCancelled={() => setPending(null)}
            onUrgentToggled={handleUrgentToggled}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </DndContext>
  );
}
```

주: `/queue-today` 백엔드 엔드포인트도 Task 13에서 함께 만든다.

```python
import pytest

from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.services import doctor_directory_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str) -> StaffContext:
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=None)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_active_doctors_excludes_inactive_staff_and_other_roles(db_conn):
    receptionist = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist"), "receptionist")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    active_doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor", department_id=dept_id, is_active=True)
    await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor", department_id=dept_id, is_active=False)
    await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist", department_id=dept_id, is_active=True)

    doctors = await doctor_directory_service.list_active_doctors(receptionist)

    assert [d["id"] for d in doctors] == [active_doctor["staff_id"]]
    assert doctors[0]["department_name"] == "내과"
```

- [ ] **Step 6: doctor_directory_service + `/doctors` 라우터 구현**

`backend/app/services/doctor_directory_service.py`:
```python
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as


async def list_active_doctors(staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select s.id, s.name, s.department_id, d.name as department_name
            from staff s
            join departments d on d.id = s.department_id
            where s.role = 'doctor' and s.is_active
            order by d.name, s.name
            """
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]
```

`backend/app/routers/doctors.py`:
```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import doctor_directory_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["doctors"])


@router.get("/doctors")
async def list_doctors(
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> list[dict]:
    return await doctor_directory_service.list_active_doctors(staff)
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { HttpResponse, http } from "msw";

import { server } from "../../test/msw/server";

vi.mock("../../lib/supabaseClient", () => ({
  supabase: { auth: { getSession: async () => ({ data: { session: { access_token: "t" } } }) } },
}));

function renderWithClient(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(<QueryClientProvider client={client}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
}

describe("WalkInRegistrationModal", () => {
  it("finds an existing patient, confirms identity twice, and adds them to the queue", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/doctors", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([{ id: "doc1", name: "김의사", department_id: "dept1", department_name: "내과" }]),
      ),
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/patients/find", () => HttpResponse.json({ patient_id: "p1" })),
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/patients/p1", () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ id: "p1", name: "홍길동", birth_date: "1985-03-01", phone: "01012345678", gender: "M", internal_notes: [] }),
      ),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/appointments", () => HttpResponse.json({ appointment_id: "a1" })),
    );
    const onRegistered = vi.fn();

    renderWithClient(<WalkInRegistrationModal onRegistered={onRegistered} onCancel={() => {}} />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("전화번호"), "01012345678");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("생년월일"), "1985-03-01");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "환자 조회" }));

    // 1차 확인: 검색 결과에서 환자 선택
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(/홍길동/)).toBeInTheDocument());
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText("담당 의사")).toBeInTheDocument());
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("담당 의사"), "doc1");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "대기 목록에 추가" }));

    // 2차 확인: 실제로 대기열에 등록되는 시점 — 아직 서버 호출이 일어나면 안 됨
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(/이 환자를 대기 목록에 추가합니다/)).toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(onRegistered).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(onRegistered).toHaveBeenCalledWith("a1"));
  });

  it("registers a brand-new patient when none is found, then adds them to the queue", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/doctors", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([{ id: "doc1", name: "김의사", department_id: "dept1", department_name: "내과" }]),
      ),
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/patients/find", () => HttpResponse.json({ patient_id: null })),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/patients", () => HttpResponse.json({ patient_id: "p2" })),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/appointments", () => HttpResponse.json({ appointment_id: "a2" })),
    );
    const onRegistered = vi.fn();

    renderWithClient(<WalkInRegistrationModal onRegistered={onRegistered} onCancel={() => {}} />);

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("전화번호"), "01099998888");

    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.getByText("조회된 환자가 없습니다. 신규 환자로 등록합니다.")).toBeInTheDocument(),
    );
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("이름"), "김신규");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("성별"), "F");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "신규 환자 등록" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText("담당 의사")).toBeInTheDocument());
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("담당 의사"), "doc1");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "대기 목록에 추가" }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(onRegistered).toHaveBeenCalledWith("a2"));

});
```

```typescript
import { apiFetch } from "./httpClient";

export type Doctor = {

```

`frontend/src/features/queue/WalkInRegistrationModal.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { createAppointment } from "../../api/appointments";
import { listDoctors } from "../../api/doctors";
import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";
import { findPatient, registerPatient } from "../../api/patients";
import { ConfirmIdentityDialog } from "../../components/ConfirmIdentityDialog";

type Props = {
  onRegistered: (appointmentId: string) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

type FoundPatient = { id: string; name: string; birth_date: string };

export function WalkInRegistrationModal({ onRegistered, onCancel }: Props) {
  const { data: doctors } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["doctors"], queryFn: listDoctors });

  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [birthDate, setBirthDate] = useState("");
  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false);
  const [foundPatient, setFoundPatient] = useState<FoundPatient | null>(null);
  const [confirmedPatientId, setConfirmedPatientId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState("");
  const [newGender, setNewGender] = useState("");
  const [doctorId, setDoctorId] = useState("");
  const [showFinalConfirm, setShowFinalConfirm] = useState(false);

  async function handleSearch() {
    const result = await findPatient(phone, birthDate);
    setSearched(true);
    if (!result.patient_id) {
      setFoundPatient(null);
      return;
    }
    const detail = await apiFetch<{ id: string; name: string; birth_date: string }>(
      `/patients/${result.patient_id}`,
    );
    setFoundPatient({ id: detail.id, name: detail.name, birth_date: detail.birth_date });
  }

  async function handleRegisterNewPatient() {
    const result = await registerPatient({ name: newName, birth_date: birthDate, gender: newGender, phone });
    setConfirmedPatientId(result.patient_id);
  }

  async function handleAddToQueue() {
    if (!confirmedPatientId || !doctorId) return;
    const doctor = doctors?.find((d) => d.id === doctorId);
    if (!doctor) return;
    const result = await createAppointment({
      account_patient_id: confirmedPatientId,
      for_patient_id: confirmedPatientId,
      department_id: doctor.department_id,
      doctor_id: doctorId,
      reason: "당일 방문",
      source: "staff",
      initial_status: "진료대기",
    });
    setShowFinalConfirm(false);
    onRegistered(result.appointment_id);
  }

  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="당일 방문 환자 등록">
      <label htmlFor="walkin-phone-input">전화번호</label>
      <input id="walkin-phone-input" value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} />
      <label htmlFor="walkin-birth-date-input">생년월일</label>
      <input id="walkin-birth-date-input" value={birthDate} onChange={(e) => setBirthDate(e.target.value)} />
      <button type="button" onClick={handleSearch}>
        환자 조회
      </button>

      {/* 1차 동명이인 확인: 기존 환자를 검색 결과에서 선택하는 시점 (설계서 섹션 6) */}
      {foundPatient && !confirmedPatientId && (
        <ConfirmIdentityDialog
          patientName={foundPatient.name}
          patientBirthDate={foundPatient.birth_date}
          actionLabel="환자로 선택합니다"
          onConfirm={() => setConfirmedPatientId(foundPatient.id)}
          onCancel={() => setFoundPatient(null)}
        />
      )}

      {searched && !foundPatient && !confirmedPatientId && (
        <div>
          <p>조회된 환자가 없습니다. 신규 환자로 등록합니다.</p>
          <label htmlFor="walkin-name-input">이름</label>
          <input id="walkin-name-input" value={newName} onChange={(e) => setNewName(e.target.value)} />
          <label htmlFor="walkin-gender-input">성별</label>
          <input id="walkin-gender-input" value={newGender} onChange={(e) => setNewGender(e.target.value)} />
          <button type="button" onClick={handleRegisterNewPatient}>
            신규 환자 등록
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {confirmedPatientId && !showFinalConfirm && (
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="walkin-doctor-select">담당 의사</label>
          <select id="walkin-doctor-select" value={doctorId} onChange={(e) => setDoctorId(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">선택하세요</option>
            {doctors?.map((doctor) => (
              <option key={doctor.id} value={doctor.id}>
                {doctor.department_name} / {doctor.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button type="button" onClick={onCancel}>
            취소
          </button>
          <button type="button" disabled={!doctorId} onClick={() => setShowFinalConfirm(true)}>
            대기 목록에 추가
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 2차 동명이인 확인: 실제로 대기열에 등록되는 시점 (설계서 섹션 6 — "검색 선택 시점 1회 + 확정 시점 1회") */}
      {showFinalConfirm && confirmedPatientId && (
        <ConfirmIdentityDialog
          patientName={foundPatient?.name ?? newName}
          patientBirthDate={foundPatient?.birth_date ?? birthDate}
          actionLabel="이 환자를 대기 목록에 추가합니다"
          onConfirm={handleAddToQueue}
          onCancel={() => setShowFinalConfirm(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: QueuePage에 "당일 방문 등록" 버튼 연결**

`frontend/src/features/queue/QueuePage.tsx` 수정 (Step 4 버전에 버튼과 모달 추가):
```tsx
import { DndContext, type DragEndEvent } from "@dnd-kit/core";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";
import { reorderQueue, setUrgentFlag } from "../../api/appointments";
import { QueueAppointment, QueueRow } from "./QueueRow";
import { WalkInRegistrationModal } from "./WalkInRegistrationModal";

export function QueuePage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: appointments } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["queue"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<QueueAppointment[]>("/queue-today"),
  });
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ id: string; newPosition: number } | null>(null);
  const [showWalkIn, setShowWalkIn] = useState(false);

  function handleDragEnd(event: DragEndEvent) {
    if (!event.over || event.active.id === event.over.id || !appointments) return;
    const newIndex = appointments.findIndex((a) => a.id === event.over!.id);
    setPending({ id: String(event.active.id), newPosition: newIndex + 1 });
  }

  async function handleReorderConfirmed(appointmentId: string, newPosition: number, reason: string) {
    await reorderQueue(appointmentId, { new_position: newPosition, reason });
    setPending(null);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["queue"] });
  }

  async function handleUrgentToggled(appointmentId: string, isUrgent: boolean) {
    const target = appointments?.find((a) => a.id === appointmentId);
    if (!target) return;
    await setUrgentFlag(appointmentId, { is_urgent: isUrgent, expected_updated_at: target.updatedAt });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["queue"] });
  }

  if (!appointments) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  return (
    <div>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowWalkIn(true)}>
        당일 방문 등록
      </button>

      {showWalkIn && (
        <WalkInRegistrationModal
          onCancel={() => setShowWalkIn(false)}
          onRegistered={() => {
            setShowWalkIn(false);
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["queue"] });
          }}
        />
      )}

      <DndContext onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}>
        <div role="list">
          {appointments.map((appointment, index) => (
            <QueueRow
              key={appointment.id}
              appointment={appointment}
              currentPosition={index + 1}
              pendingNewPosition={pending?.id === appointment.id ? pending.newPosition : null}
              onReorderConfirmed={handleReorderConfirmed}
              onReorderCancelled={() => setPending(null)}
              onUrgentToggled={handleUrgentToggled}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </DndContext>
    </div>
  );
}
```

주: `/queue-today` 백엔드 엔드포인트는 Task 13에서 함께 만든다. `POST /patients`, `GET /patients/find`는 Task 6, `POST /appointments`는 1단계 서비스를 그대로 사용한다.

## Task 10: 환자 상세 화면 (`/patients/:id`)

> **[2026-07-28 재검증 — 1차 문서 반영]** 1차 정합성 검토(우선4)가 지적한 대로, 환자 상세는 이름/생년월일/전화/성별/내부메모만 보여줘서 접수직원이 예약/방문이력·사전문진 이력·과거 진료기록·가족관계를 확인하려면 다른 화면을 오가야 했다. 아래 Step 2.5에서 4개 섹션(예약·방문이력, 사전문진 응답 이력, 과거 진료기록, 가족관계)을 추가한다. 백엔드는 Task 13 Step 9에서 `get_patient_detail`을 확장하고 `GET /patients/{id}/visits`·`GET /patients/{id}/family`를 신설한다. 접근 권한: 접수직원·관리자는 `patients` 테이블 자체가 그 역할에 전체 공개돼 있어(1단계 Task 3 RLS) 모든 환자를 볼 수 있고, 의사가 이 화면에 접근하는 경로는 이 태스크 범위 밖(의사는 Task 11 `DoctorConsolePage`에서 `doctor_can_view_appointment()`로 범위가 좁혀진 화면만 쓴다)이라 별도 처리하지 않는다.

- Create: `frontend/src/features/patients/PatientDetailPage.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/patients/PatientVisitHistorySection.tsx`([2026-07-28 재검증])

- Produces: `PatientDetailPage` — `/patients/:id` 라우트, `<PatientVisitHistorySection patientId />`([2026-07-28 재검증] 예약/방문이력을 상태별로 표시), `<PatientFamilySection patientId />`([2026-07-28 재검증] `patient_family_links` 기반 가족관계 목록, 1단계 Task 4 스키마)

```tsx
}));

function renderWithProviders(patientId: string) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[`/patients/${patientId}`]}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/patients/:id" element={<PatientDetailPage />} />
        </Routes>
      </MemoryRouter>

  );
}

});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: PatientDetailPage 구현**

`frontend/src/features/patients/PatientDetailPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type PatientDetail = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  birth_date: string;
  phone: string;
  gender: string;
  internal_notes: { id: string; content: string; staff_name: string; created_at: string }[];
};

export function PatientDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["patient", id],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<PatientDetail>(`/patients/${id}`),
    enabled: Boolean(id),
  });

  if (!data) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  return (
    <div>
      <h1>{data.name}</h1>
      <p>{data.birth_date}</p>
      <p>{data.phone}</p>
      <section aria-label="내부 메모">
        {data.internal_notes.map((note) => (
          <p key={note.id}>{note.content}</p>
        ))}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

주: `GET /patients/{id}` 상세 조회 엔드포인트(내부메모/사전문진/진료기록 포함)는 백엔드에 아직 없다 — Task 13에서 통계·조회 계열 엔드포인트와 함께 추가한다.

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type Visit = { id: string; slot_date: string; status: string; doctor_name: string };

export function PatientVisitHistorySection({ patientId }: { patientId: string }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["patient-visits", patientId],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<Visit[]>(`/patients/${patientId}/visits`),
  });

      {data.map((v) => (
        <p key={v.id}>
          {v.slot_date} {v.doctor_name} — {v.status}
        </p>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type FamilyLink = { id: string; family_patient_name: string; relation: string };

export function PatientFamilySection({ patientId }: { patientId: string }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["patient-family", patientId],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<FamilyLink[]>(`/patients/${patientId}/family`),
  });
  if (!data || data.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-label="가족관계">
      {data.map((f) => (
        <p key={f.id}>
          {f.family_patient_name} ({f.relation})
        </p>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

## Task 11: 의사의 진료 화면 (`/doctor/console`)

> **[2026-07-28 재검증 — 1차 문서 반영]** 1차 정합성 검토(우선4)가 지적한 대로, `DoctorConsolePage`가 증상/진단/처치 입력폼과 진료문구 불러오기뿐이어서 의사가 진료를 시작하기 전 참고해야 할 정보(오늘 본인 대기목록·이 예약의 사전문진 응답·이 환자의 과거 진료기록·이 진료기록의 수정이력)를 화면 안에서 볼 수 없었다. 아래 Step 4.5에서 4개 패널(`DoctorQueuePanel`, `AppointmentQuestionnairePanel`, `PatientHistoryPanel`, `RecordRevisionHistoryPanel`)을 추가한다. 새 조회는 모두 Task 13의 `dashboard_service`를 확장(Task 13 Step 9 참고)하거나 1단계에서 이미 만든 `GET /medical-records/by-appointment/{appointment_id}`·`GET /medical-records/{record_id}/revisions`(둘 다 `foundation-auth-data-model.md` Task 8, [정합성 검토 R5-08])를 그대로 재사용한다. 접근 범위는 모두 1단계 `doctor_can_view_appointment()` RLS를 그대로 통과하므로(서비스가 `acquire_as(staff.auth_user_id)`로 세션을 열기 때문에), 다른 의사의 예약 데이터는 애초에 쿼리 결과에 잡히지 않는다 — 서비스 코드에서 별도 필터링을 하지 않아도 된다.

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { HttpResponse, http } from "msw";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { server } from "../../test/msw/server";
import { QuickPhrasePicker } from "./QuickPhrasePicker";

function renderWithClient(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(<QueryClientProvider client={client}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
}

describe("QuickPhrasePicker", () => {
  it("inserts the selected phrase text", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/quick-phrases", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([{ id: "ph1", text: "충분한 휴식과 수분 섭취를 권장합니다" }]),
      ),
    );
    const onInsert = vi.fn();

    renderWithClient(<QuickPhrasePicker doctorId="doc1" onInsert={onInsert} />);

    const button = await screen.findByText("충분한 휴식과 수분 섭취를 권장합니다");
    await userEvent.click(button);

    expect(onInsert).toHaveBeenCalledWith("충분한 휴식과 수분 섭취를 권장합니다");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: QuickPhrasePicker 구현**

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/QuickPhrasePicker.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { listQuickPhrases } from "../../api/quickPhrases";

export function QuickPhrasePicker({ doctorId, onInsert }: { doctorId: string; onInsert: (text: string) => void }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["quick-phrases", doctorId],
    queryFn: () => listQuickPhrases(doctorId),
  });

  if (!data) return null;

  return (
    <div>
      {data.map((phrase) => (
        <button key={phrase.id} type="button" onClick={() => onInsert(phrase.text)}>
          {phrase.text}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: DoctorConsolePage 구현 (조합 컴포넌트, 별도 테스트 없이 하위 컴포넌트 테스트로 커버)**

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/DoctorConsolePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";

import { completeRecord, saveDraft } from "../../api/medicalRecords";
import { QuickPhrasePicker } from "./QuickPhrasePicker";

export function DoctorConsolePage({ doctorId, appointmentId }: { doctorId: string; appointmentId: string }) {
  const [symptoms, setSymptoms] = useState("");
  const [diagnosis, setDiagnosis] = useState("");
  const [treatment, setTreatment] = useState("");
  const [patientVisibleNotes, setPatientVisibleNotes] = useState("");
  const [recordId, setRecordId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showCompleteConfirm, setShowCompleteConfirm] = useState(false);

  async function handleSaveDraft() {
    const result = await saveDraft({
      appointment_id: appointmentId,
      symptoms,
      diagnosis,
      treatment,
      patient_visible_notes: patientVisibleNotes,
    });
    setRecordId(result.record_id);
  }

  async function handleComplete() {
    if (!recordId) return;
    await completeRecord(recordId);
    setShowCompleteConfirm(false);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <textarea aria-label="증상" value={symptoms} onChange={(e) => setSymptoms(e.target.value)} />
      <textarea aria-label="진단" value={diagnosis} onChange={(e) => setDiagnosis(e.target.value)} />
      <textarea aria-label="처치" value={treatment} onChange={(e) => setTreatment(e.target.value)} />
      <QuickPhrasePicker doctorId={doctorId} onInsert={(text) => setTreatment((prev) => `${prev}${text}`)} />
      <textarea
        aria-label="환자 공개 안내"
        value={patientVisibleNotes}
        onChange={(e) => setPatientVisibleNotes(e.target.value)}
      />
      <button type="button" onClick={handleSaveDraft}>
        임시저장
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowCompleteConfirm(true)}>
        완료
      </button>

      {showCompleteConfirm && (
        <div role="dialog">
          <p>진료기록을 완료 처리하시겠습니까? 완료 후에는 사유 입력 없이 수정할 수 없습니다.</p>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowCompleteConfirm(false)}>
            취소
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleComplete}>
            확인
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

각 패널 구현(단순 조회+렌더링, 기존 `QuickPhrasePicker`와 동일한 `useQuery` 패턴):

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/DoctorQueuePanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type QueueRow = { id: string; patient_name: string; wait_minutes: number; queue_position: number | null };

// [2026-07-28 재검증] /queue-today는 RLS(doctor_can_view_appointment)로 이미 "본인 담당 예약"만 반환하므로
// 여기서 doctorId로 다시 필터링하지 않는다 — 클라이언트 필터를 추가하면 오히려 서버 신뢰 경계를 흐린다.
export function DoctorQueuePanel({ currentAppointmentId }: { currentAppointmentId: string }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["queue-today"], queryFn: () => apiFetch<QueueRow[]>("/queue-today") });
  if (!data) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-label="오늘 대기 목록">
      {data.map((row) => (
        <p key={row.id} aria-current={row.id === currentAppointmentId ? "true" : undefined}>
          {row.queue_position ?? "-"}번 {row.patient_name} ({Math.round(row.wait_minutes)}분 대기)
        </p>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/AppointmentQuestionnairePanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type Questionnaire = { submitted_at: string | null; answers: Record<string, string> } | null;

export function AppointmentQuestionnairePanel({ appointmentId }: { appointmentId: string }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["questionnaire", appointmentId],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<Questionnaire>(`/questionnaire-responses/by-appointment/${appointmentId}`),
  });
  if (!data) return <section aria-label="사전문진"><p>제출된 사전문진이 없습니다.</p></section>;
  return (
    <section aria-label="사전문진">
      {Object.entries(data.answers).map(([question, answer]) => (
        <p key={question}>
          {question}: {answer}
        </p>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/PatientHistoryPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type PastRecord = { id: string; appointment_date: string; diagnosis: string };

// [2026-07-28 재검증] 백엔드가 현재 예약(excludeAppointmentId)을 이미 제외하고 반환하므로
// (Task 13 Step 9 get_patient_medical_history), 여기서는 그대로 나열만 한다.
export function PatientHistoryPanel({ patientId }: { patientId: string; excludeAppointmentId: string }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["patient-medical-records", patientId],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<PastRecord[]>(`/patients/${patientId}/medical-records`),
  });
  if (!data) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-label="과거 진료기록">
      {data.map((r) => (
        <p key={r.id}>
          {r.appointment_date} — {r.diagnosis || "(진단 미기재)"}
        </p>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/RecordRevisionHistoryPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

type Revision = { id: string; reason: string; revised_at: string };

export function RecordRevisionHistoryPanel({ recordId }: { recordId: string | null }) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["record-revisions", recordId],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<Revision[]>(`/medical-records/${recordId}/revisions`),
    enabled: Boolean(recordId),
  });
  if (!recordId || !data || data.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-label="수정이력">
      {data.map((rev) => (
        <p key={rev.id}>
          {rev.revised_at} — {rev.reason}
        </p>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

## Task 12: 관리자 — 영향받는 예약 확인 다이얼로그 + 운영 통계 화면

> 원래 이 태스크에 있던 일정관리 화면(`SchedulePage`)은 하드코딩된 버튼과 빈 섹션뿐인 뼈대여서 고객 요구(진료과 CRUD, 의사별 스케줄, 특정일 예외 등록)를 충족하지 못했다. 실제 구현은 백엔드 Task 17 + 프론트엔드 Task 18로 분리·승격했고, 이 태스크는 공용 다이얼로그(`AffectedAppointmentsDialog`)와 운영 통계 화면만 담당한다.

> **[2026-07-28 재검증 — 1차 문서 반영]** 1차 정합성 검토 우선5는 시간대별·장기대기 외에 과별/의사별 분해, 드릴다운(지표 클릭 시 상세 목록), CSV 내보내기가 빠졌다고 지적했다. 시간대별/장기대기는 이미 [정합성 검토 R2-04]/[R2-03]으로 반영돼 있어 중복 작업하지 않고, 아래 Step 6에서 나머지 3가지만 채운다: 과/의사별 브레이크다운 표(Task 13 Step 10의 `get_stats(..., by=)` 소비), 지표 클릭 시 상세 목록 모달(드릴다운), CSV 다운로드 버튼(클라이언트 사이드 변환).

`frontend/src/features/admin/AffectedAppointmentsDialog.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { HttpResponse, http } from "msw";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { server } from "../../test/msw/server";
import { AffectedAppointmentsDialog } from "./AffectedAppointmentsDialog";

function renderWithClient(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(<QueryClientProvider client={client}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
}

describe("AffectedAppointmentsDialog", () => {
  it("defaults all affected appointments to selected and confirms with selected ids", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/appointments/affected", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([
          { id: "a1", reason: null, patient_name: "홍길동", masked_phone: "010-****-5678", slot_date: "2026-08-01", start_time: "09:00:00" },
          { id: "a2", reason: null, patient_name: "이몽룡", masked_phone: "010-****-8888", slot_date: "2026-08-01", start_time: "10:00:00" },
        ]),
      ),
    );
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();

    renderWithClient(<AffectedAppointmentsDialog doctorId="doc1" onConfirm={onConfirm} onCancel={() => {}} />);

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("홍길동")).toBeInTheDocument());
```

`frontend/src/features/admin/AffectedAppointmentsDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { listAffectedAppointments, type CandidateException } from "../../api/appointments";

type Props = {
  doctorId: string;
  onConfirm: (selectedIds: string[]) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
  // [정합성 검토 R3-02] 저장 전 미리보기 대상 후보 예외값. 없으면(하위 호환) 이미 저장된 예외 기준으로 조회한다.
  candidateException?: CandidateException;
};

export function AffectedAppointmentsDialog({ doctorId, onConfirm, onCancel, candidateException }: Props) {
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["affected-appointments", doctorId, candidateException],
    queryFn: () => listAffectedAppointments(doctorId, candidateException),
  });
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string> | null>(null);

  if (!data) return <p>확인 중입니다...</p>;

  const selectedIds = selected ?? new Set(data.map((a) => a.id));
  if (selected === null && data.length > 0) setSelected(selectedIds);

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div role="dialog">
        <p>영향받는 예약이 없습니다.</p>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onConfirm([])}>
          저장
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  function toggle(id: string) {
    const next = new Set(selectedIds);
    if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
    else next.add(id);
    setSelected(next);
  }

  return (
    <div role="dialog">
      <p>영향받는 예약 {data.length}건</p>
      {data.map((appointment) => (
        <label key={appointment.id}>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={selectedIds.has(appointment.id)}
            onChange={() => toggle(appointment.id)}
          />
          {appointment.patient_name} ({appointment.masked_phone}) — {appointment.slot_date} {appointment.start_time}
        </label>
      ))}
      <button type="button" onClick={onCancel}>
        취소
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onConfirm(Array.from(selectedIds))}>
        저장
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

// [정합성 검토 R2-04] 필드명에 집계 기준일을 드러낸다(예약생성일 기준인지, 실제 사건 발생일 기준인지).
type StatsSummary = {
  reserved_by_created_date: number;
  cancelled_by_event_date: number;
  no_show_by_event_date: number;
  visited_by_event_date: number;
  average_wait_minutes_by_wait_start_date: number;
  app_booking_ratio_by_created_date: number;
  visits_by_hour: Record<number, number>;
};

export function StatsPage() {
  const [from, setFrom] = useState("");
  const [to, setTo] = useState("");
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["stats", from, to],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<StatsSummary>(`/stats?from=${from}&to=${to}`),
    enabled: Boolean(from && to),
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <input aria-label="시작일" type="date" value={from} onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)} />
      <input aria-label="종료일" type="date" value={to} onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value)} />
      {data && (
        <table>
          <tbody>
            <tr>
              <td>예약(생성일 기준)</td>
              <td>{data.reserved_by_created_date}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>취소(취소 발생일 기준)</td>
              <td>{data.cancelled_by_event_date}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>부도(부도 발생일 기준)</td>
              <td>{data.no_show_by_event_date}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>방문(방문 발생일 기준)</td>
              <td>{data.visited_by_event_date}</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>평균 대기시간(분)</td>
              <td>{data.average_wait_minutes_by_wait_start_date}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      {data && (
        <table aria-label="시간대별 방문 환자 수">
          <thead>
            <tr><th>시간대</th><th>방문 환자 수</th></tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {Object.entries(data.visits_by_hour).map(([hour, count]) => (
              <tr key={hour}>
                <td>{hour}시</td>
                <td>{count}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```
