`StatsPage.tsx`를 다음으로 교체(`breakdown` 표, 지표 클릭 시 드릴다운, CSV 다운로드 버튼 추가):
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";
import { StatsDrilldownModal } from "./StatsDrilldownModal";

type StatsSummary = {
  reserved_by_created_date: number;
  cancelled_by_event_date: number;
  no_show_by_event_date: number;
  visited_by_event_date: number;
  average_wait_minutes_by_wait_start_date: number;
  app_booking_ratio_by_created_date: number;
  visits_by_hour: Record<number, number>;
  breakdown: { department_id?: string; doctor_id?: string; visited_by_event_date: number; cancelled_by_event_date: number }[];
};

const METRIC_ROWS: { key: keyof StatsSummary; label: string; metric: string }[] = [
  { key: "reserved_by_created_date", label: "예약(생성일 기준)", metric: "reserved" },
  { key: "cancelled_by_event_date", label: "취소(취소 발생일 기준)", metric: "cancelled" },
  { key: "no_show_by_event_date", label: "부도(부도 발생일 기준)", metric: "no_show" },
  { key: "visited_by_event_date", label: "방문(방문 발생일 기준)", metric: "visited" },
];

function downloadCsv(data: StatsSummary) {
  const rows = [
    ["지표", "값"],
    ...METRIC_ROWS.map((m) => [m.label, String(data[m.key])]),
    ["평균 대기시간(분)", String(data.average_wait_minutes_by_wait_start_date)],
  ];
  const csv = rows.map((r) => r.join(",")).join("\n");
  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = "stats.csv";
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function StatsPage() {
  const [from, setFrom] = useState("");
  const [to, setTo] = useState("");
  const [breakdownBy, setBreakdownBy] = useState<"department" | "doctor">("department");
  const [drilldown, setDrilldown] = useState<{ metric: string; label: string } | null>(null);
  const { data } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["stats", from, to, breakdownBy],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<StatsSummary>(`/stats?from=${from}&to=${to}&by=${breakdownBy}`),
    enabled: Boolean(from && to),
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <input aria-label="시작일" type="date" value={from} onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)} />
      <input aria-label="종료일" type="date" value={to} onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value)} />
      {data && (
        <table>
          <tbody>
            {METRIC_ROWS.map((m) => (
              <tr key={m.metric}>
                <td>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => setDrilldown({ metric: m.metric, label: m.label })}>
                    {m.label}
                  </button>
                </td>
                <td>{data[m.key] as number}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
            <tr>
              <td>평균 대기시간(분)</td>
              <td>{data.average_wait_minutes_by_wait_start_date}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      {data && (
        <table aria-label="시간대별 방문 환자 수">
          <thead>
            <tr><th>시간대</th><th>방문 환자 수</th></tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {Object.entries(data.visits_by_hour).map(([hour, count]) => (
              <tr key={hour}>
                <td>{hour}시</td>
                <td>{count}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      {data && (
        <section aria-label="과/의사별 통계">
          <label>
            기준
            <select value={breakdownBy} onChange={(e) => setBreakdownBy(e.target.value as "department" | "doctor")}>
              <option value="department">진료과별</option>
              <option value="doctor">의사별</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <table>
            <tbody>
              {data.breakdown.map((row) => (
                <tr key={row.department_id ?? row.doctor_id}>
                  <td>{row.department_id ?? row.doctor_id}</td>
                  <td>방문 {row.visited_by_event_date}</td>
                  <td>취소 {row.cancelled_by_event_date}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </section>
      )}
      {data && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => downloadCsv(data)}>
          CSV 다운로드
        </button>
      )}
      {drilldown && (
        <StatsDrilldownModal
          metric={drilldown.metric}
          label={drilldown.label}
          from={from}
          to={to}
          onClose={() => setDrilldown(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

주: `data.breakdown`의 `department_id`/`doctor_id`는 UUID 그대로다. 실제 화면에는 이름이 필요하므로, 다음 반복에서 `/departments`·`/doctors` 목록(Task 17에서 이미 만든 `list_departments`/`list_calendar_slots`용 의사 목록)과 조인해 이름으로 치환하는 매핑 작업이 남아있다 — 이번 재검증에서는 데이터 자체가 조회 가능해지는 것과 클릭 동선을 우선 확보한다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성 — get_today_summary**

`backend/tests/test_dashboard_service.py`:
```python
import pytest

from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.services import dashboard_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str) -> StaffContext:
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=None)


async def _seed_appointment(db_conn, doctor_id, receptionist_id, status, minutes_ago=0):
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    appointment_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        """
        insert into appointments
            (account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $1, $2, $3, $4, 'staff', $5)
        returning id
        """,
        patient_id, dept_id, doctor_id, status, receptionist_id,
    )
    if minutes_ago:
        # [정합성 검토 R2-03] 대기시간은 상태이력의 '진료대기' 전이 changed_at을 쓴다.
        # INSERT 트리거가 실제 now()로 남긴 이력 행을, 테스트가 원하는 경과시간만큼 되돌린다.
        await db_conn.execute(
            """
            update appointment_status_history
            set changed_at = now() - ($1 || ' minutes')::interval
            where appointment_id = $2 and to_status = $3
            """,
            minutes_ago, appointment_id, status,
        )
    return appointment_id


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_today_summary_counts_by_status(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    receptionist = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")

    await _seed_appointment(db_conn, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist["staff_id"], "진료대기", minutes_ago=45)
    await _seed_appointment(db_conn, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist["staff_id"], "진료완료")

    summary = await dashboard_service.get_today_summary(admin)

    assert summary["waiting"] == 1
    assert summary["completed"] == 1
    assert len(summary["long_wait_patients"]) == 1
    assert summary["long_wait_patients"][0]["patient_name"] == "홍길동"

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_long_wait_survives_queue_reorder(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R2-03] 순서 재배치(메모성 이력)는 대기시작시각을 초기화하지 않는다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    receptionist = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")

    appointment_id = await _seed_appointment(db_conn, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist["staff_id"], "진료대기", minutes_ago=45)
    other_id = await _seed_appointment(db_conn, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist["staff_id"], "진료대기", minutes_ago=1)

    # 순서 재배치: from_status = to_status인 메모성 이력만 남긴다(1단계 staff_can_insert_note_history 정책).
    await db_conn.execute("update appointments set queue_position = 1 where id = $1", appointment_id)
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into appointment_status_history (appointment_id, from_status, to_status, changed_by, reason) values ($1, '진료대기', '진료대기', $2, '순서변경')",
        appointment_id, admin.id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute("update appointments set queue_position = 2 where id = $1", other_id)

    queue = await dashboard_service.get_queue_today(admin)
    row = next(r for r in queue if r["id"] == appointment_id)
    assert row["wait_minutes"] >= 44  # 메모 삽입 이후에도 45분 전 대기시작시각이 유지됨

    summary = await dashboard_service.get_today_summary(admin)
    assert len(summary["long_wait_patients"]) == 1
    assert summary["long_wait_patients"][0]["appointment_id"] == appointment_id
```

`backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py`:
```python
from datetime import date
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.masking import mask_birth_date  # [정합성 검토 R5-06]
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as


async def get_today_summary(staff: StaffContext) -> dict:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        threshold = await conn.fetchval("select long_wait_threshold_minutes from hospital_settings")

        # [정합성 검토 R2-07] "오늘 현황"은 슬롯이 있는 예약이면 slot_date, 슬롯 없는 당일방문(워크인)이면
        # 접수(생성) 날짜를 "오늘"로 삼는다. updated_at/created_at 혼용으로 오늘 만든 미래예약이나
        # 오늘 수정한 과거예약이 섞여 들어오던 문제를 없앤다. 시간대는 병원 기준 Asia/Seoul로 통일한다.
        counts = await conn.fetchrow(
            """
            select
                count(*) filter (where status in ('예약신청', '예약확정', '도착', '진료대기', '진료중', '진료완료')) as total_reserved,
                count(*) filter (where status = '도착') as arrived,
                count(*) filter (where status = '진료대기') as waiting,
                count(*) filter (where status = '진료중') as in_progress,
                count(*) filter (where status = '진료완료') as completed,
                count(*) filter (where status in ('환자취소', '병원취소', '예약부도')) as cancelled_or_no_show
            from appointments a
            left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
            where (s.slot_date = (now() at time zone 'Asia/Seoul')::date)
               or (a.slot_id is null and (a.created_at at time zone 'Asia/Seoul')::date = (now() at time zone 'Asia/Seoul')::date)
            """
        )

        # [정합성 검토 R2-03] 대기 시작 시각은 '진료대기'로 전이된 시점(상태이력)을 쓴다.
        # 순서 재배치·주의 표시 메모는 from_status = to_status인 이력 행만 남기므로(1단계 정책),
        # 이 서브쿼리는 실제 상태전이 시점만 잡아내고 메모로 인해 갱신되지 않는다.
        long_wait_rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select a.id as appointment_id, p.name as patient_name
            from appointments a
            join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
            join lateral (
                select h.changed_at as waiting_started_at
                from appointment_status_history h
                where h.appointment_id = a.id and h.to_status = '진료대기' and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
                order by h.changed_at desc
                limit 1
            ) w on true
            where a.status = '진료대기'
              and now() - w.waiting_started_at >= ($1 || ' minutes')::interval
            """,
            threshold,
        )

        urgent_rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select a.id as appointment_id, p.name as patient_name
            from appointments a
            join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
            where a.is_urgent_flag and a.status in ('도착', '진료대기')
            """
        )

    return {
        "total_reserved": counts["total_reserved"],
        "arrived": counts["arrived"],
        "waiting": counts["waiting"],
        "in_progress": counts["in_progress"],
        "completed": counts["completed"],
        "cancelled_or_no_show": counts["cancelled_or_no_show"],
        "long_wait_patients": [dict(row) for row in long_wait_rows],
        "urgent_patients": [dict(row) for row in urgent_rows],
        "pending_inquiries_count": 0,
        "affected_appointments_count": 0,
    }


async def get_queue_today(staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    # [정합성 검토 R2-03] 대기시간은 '진료대기' 전이 시각(waiting_started_at) 기준으로 계산한다.
    # 순서 변경·주의 표시 갱신(from_status = to_status인 메모 이력)은 이 값에 영향을 주지 않는다.
    # [2026-07-28 재검증] patient_id를 함께 반환한다 — Task 18의 DoctorConsoleRoute가 이 값으로
    # PatientHistoryPanel(Task 11)에 넘길 patientId를 얻는다(별도 조회 없이 대기목록 조회에 편승).
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select a.id, a.for_patient_id as patient_id, p.name as patient_name, p.birth_date as raw_birth_date,
                   a.is_urgent_flag, w.waiting_started_at,
                   extract(epoch from (now() - w.waiting_started_at)) / 60 as wait_minutes
            from appointments a
            join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
            left join lateral (
                select h.changed_at as waiting_started_at
                from appointment_status_history h
                where h.appointment_id = a.id and h.to_status = '진료대기' and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
                order by h.changed_at desc
                limit 1
            ) w on true
            where a.status in ('도착', '진료대기', '진료중')
            order by coalesce(a.queue_position, 999999), w.waiting_started_at nulls last
            """
        )
    # [정합성 검토 R5-06] 목록 응답은 masked_birth_date만 담는다(원본 birth_date 미포함).
    return [
        {**{k: v for k, v in dict(row).items() if k != "raw_birth_date"}, "masked_birth_date": mask_birth_date(row["raw_birth_date"])}
        for row in rows
    ]


async def get_patient_detail(patient_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> dict:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        patient = await conn.fetchrow("select * from patients where id = $1", patient_id)
        notes = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select n.id, n.content, s.name as staff_name, n.created_at
            from patient_internal_notes n
            join staff s on s.id = n.staff_id
            where n.patient_id = $1
            order by n.created_at desc
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type) values ($1, $2, 'patient_detail')",
            staff.id, patient_id,
        )

    return {
        "id": patient["id"],
        "name": patient["name"],
        "birth_date": str(patient["birth_date"]),
        "phone": patient["phone"],
        "gender": patient["gender"],
        "internal_notes": [dict(row) for row in notes],
    }


async def get_stats(from_date: date, to_date: date, staff: StaffContext) -> dict:
    # [정합성 검토 R2-04] 지표마다 실제 사건이 발생한 날짜를 기준으로 집계한다(생성일 단일 기준 아님).
    # reserved/app_booking_ratio: 신규 유입이므로 created_at 기준.
    # visited/cancelled/no_show: 각 상태로 실제 전이된 날짜(appointment_status_history.changed_at) 기준.
    # average_wait_minutes: '진료대기' 전이(waiting_started_at) 발생일 기준.
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        reserved = await conn.fetchval(
            "select count(*) from appointments where created_at::date between $1 and $2",
            from_date, to_date,
        )
        cancelled = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            select count(distinct h.appointment_id)
            from appointment_status_history h
            where h.to_status in ('환자취소', '병원취소')
              and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
              and h.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
            """,
            from_date, to_date,
        )
        no_show = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            select count(distinct h.appointment_id)
            from appointment_status_history h
            where h.to_status = '예약부도'
              and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
              and h.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
            """,
            from_date, to_date,
        )
        visited = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            select count(distinct h.appointment_id)
            from appointment_status_history h
            where h.to_status = '진료완료'
              and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
              and h.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
            """,
            from_date, to_date,
        )
        avg_wait = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            select avg(extract(epoch from (h2.changed_at - h1.changed_at)) / 60)
            from appointment_status_history h1
            join appointment_status_history h2
                on h2.appointment_id = h1.appointment_id and h2.from_status = '진료대기' and h2.to_status = '진료중'
            where h1.to_status = '진료대기'
              and h1.from_status is distinct from h1.to_status
              and h1.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
            """,
            from_date, to_date,
        )
        app_ratio = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            select coalesce(count(*) filter (where source = 'app'), 0)::float / nullif(count(*), 0)
            from appointments
            where created_at::date between $1 and $2
            """,
            from_date, to_date,
        )
        # [정합성 검토 R2-04] 시간대별 방문(진료완료) 환자 수 — 슬롯의 start_time 기준.
        visits_by_hour_rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select extract(hour from s.start_time)::int as hour, count(distinct h.appointment_id) as count
            from appointment_status_history h
            join appointments a on a.id = h.appointment_id
            join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
            where h.to_status = '진료완료'
              and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
              and h.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
            group by hour
            order by hour
            """,
            from_date, to_date,
        )

    # [정합성 검토 R2-04] 필드명에 어떤 사건 날짜를 기준으로 집계했는지 드러낸다 —
    # 이전에는 "reserved"/"cancelled"처럼 이름만 봐서는 기준일이 예약생성일인지 상태전이일인지 알 수 없었다.
    return {
        "reserved_by_created_date": reserved,
        "cancelled_by_event_date": cancelled,
        "no_show_by_event_date": no_show,
        "visited_by_event_date": visited,
        "average_wait_minutes_by_wait_start_date": round(avg_wait, 1) if avg_wait is not None else 0,
        "app_booking_ratio_by_created_date": round(app_ratio, 2) if app_ratio is not None else 0,
        "visits_by_hour": {row["hour"]: row["count"] for row in visits_by_hour_rows},
    }
```

```python
from datetime import date
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import dashboard_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["dashboard"])


@router.get("/today-summary")
async def today_summary(
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> dict:
    return await dashboard_service.get_today_summary(staff)


@router.get("/queue-today")
async def queue_today(
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> list[dict]:
    return await dashboard_service.get_queue_today(staff)


@router.get("/patients/{patient_id}")
async def patient_detail(
    patient_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> dict:
    return await dashboard_service.get_patient_detail(patient_id, staff)


@router.get("/stats")
async def stats(
    from_: date,
    to: date,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> dict:
    return await dashboard_service.get_stats(from_, to, staff)
```

주: `patients` 라우터(Task 6)의 `prefix="/patients"`와 이 `dashboard` 라우터의 `/patients/{patient_id}`가 경로상 겹치므로, `main.py`에 `dashboard.router`를 `patients.router`보다 먼저 등록해 `/patients/find`가 `{patient_id}`로 잘못 매칭되지 않게 한다(FastAPI는 등록 순서대로 라우트를 매칭하며, `find`가 UUID 파서를 통과하지 못하면 404가 나므로 실제로는 순서 무관하게 동작하지만, 명시적으로 `dashboard.router`를 나중에 등록해 `/patients/find`가 항상 먼저 매칭되도록 한다).

`backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py`의 `get_patient_detail`을 아래로 교체하고 4개 함수를 추가:
```python
async def get_patient_detail(patient_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> dict:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        patient = await conn.fetchrow("select * from patients where id = $1", patient_id)
        notes = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select n.id, n.content, s.name as staff_name, n.created_at
            from patient_internal_notes n
            join staff s on s.id = n.staff_id
            where n.patient_id = $1
            order by n.created_at desc
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
        # [2026-07-28 재검증] 사전문진 이력 — 이 환자가 진료대상(for_patient_id)이었던 예약들의 응답.
        questionnaire_rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select qr.appointment_id, qr.submitted_at, qr.answers
            from questionnaire_responses qr
            join appointments a on a.id = qr.appointment_id
            where a.for_patient_id = $1
            order by qr.submitted_at desc nulls last
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
        # [2026-07-28 재검증] 과거 진료기록 — 완료된 기록만(진료중 초안은 목록에 노출하지 않는다).
        record_rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select mr.id, s.slot_date as appointment_date, mr.diagnosis
            from medical_records mr
            join appointments a on a.id = mr.appointment_id
            left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
            where a.for_patient_id = $1 and mr.is_completed
            order by s.slot_date desc nulls last
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type) values ($1, $2, 'patient_detail')",
            staff.id, patient_id,
        )

    return {
        "id": patient["id"],
        "name": patient["name"],
        "birth_date": str(patient["birth_date"]),
        "phone": patient["phone"],
        "gender": patient["gender"],
        "internal_notes": [dict(row) for row in notes],
        "questionnaire_history": [dict(row) for row in questionnaire_rows],
        "medical_record_history": [dict(row) for row in record_rows],
    }


async def get_patient_visits(patient_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    # [2026-07-28 재검증] 예약/방문이력 — 상태별로 그대로 나열(취소/부도 포함, 필터링은 프론트에서).
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select a.id, s.slot_date, a.status, d.name as doctor_name
            from appointments a
            left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
            left join staff d on d.id = a.doctor_id
            where a.for_patient_id = $1
            order by coalesce(s.slot_date, a.created_at::date) desc
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def get_appointment_questionnaire(appointment_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> dict | None:
    # [2026-07-28 재검증] questionnaire_responses RLS(assigned_doctor_can_read_responses, 1단계 Task 7)를
    # 그대로 통과시킨다 — 담당의가 아닌 의사가 조회하면 결과가 비어 None을 반환한다.
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select submitted_at, answers from questionnaire_responses where appointment_id = $1",
            appointment_id,
        )
    return dict(row) if row else None


async def get_patient_medical_history(
    patient_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext, exclude_appointment_id: UUID | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select mr.id, s.slot_date as appointment_date, mr.diagnosis
            from medical_records mr
            join appointments a on a.id = mr.appointment_id
            left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
            where a.for_patient_id = $1 and mr.is_completed
              and ($2::uuid is null or a.id != $2)
            order by s.slot_date desc nulls last
            """,
            patient_id, exclude_appointment_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def get_patient_family(patient_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    # [2026-07-28 재검증] [정합성 검토 R5-02]가 도입한 is_active만 조회 — 해제된 연결은 제외한다.
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select l.id, p.name as family_patient_name, l.relation
            from patient_family_links l
            join patients p on p.id = l.family_patient_id
            where l.account_patient_id = $1 and l.is_active
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]
```

`backend/app/routers/dashboard.py`에 라우트 추가:

`backend/app/routers/questionnaire_responses.py`(신규 라우터, 의사 전용 좁은 조회). 경로를 `GET /appointments/{appointment_id}/questionnaire`가 아니라 `GET /questionnaire-responses/by-appointment/{appointment_id}`로 지은 이유: 3단계 `patient-app.md`(Task — `patient_questionnaire_service`)가 환자용으로 이미 `GET /appointments/{appointment_id}/questionnaire`를 `PatientContext` 인증으로 등록해뒀다. 같은 백엔드(`backend/app/main.py`)에 두 라우터를 모두 올리면 경로가 완전히 겹쳐 먼저 등록된 쪽만 응답하고 다른 쪽은 죽은 코드가 되므로, 직원용은 `medical-records/by-appointment/{id}`와 동일한 명명 규칙으로 경로를 분리한다:
```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import dashboard_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["questionnaire"])


@router.get("/questionnaire-responses/by-appointment/{appointment_id}")
async def appointment_questionnaire(
    appointment_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> dict | None:
    return await dashboard_service.get_appointment_questionnaire(appointment_id, staff)
```

`backend/app/main.py`에 라우터 등록 추가:
```python
from app.routers import appointments, dashboard, doctors, me, medical_records, patients, questionnaire_responses, quick_phrases, staff

app.include_router(questionnaire_responses.router)
```

Run: `cd backend && pytest tests/test_dashboard_service.py -v`
Expected: FAIL (`by` 인자, `breakdown` 필드 없음)

`get_stats`의 시그니처와 반환값을 아래로 교체(기존 집계 쿼리는 그대로 유지하고 `by`가 주어졌을 때만 `breakdown`을 추가 계산):
```python
async def get_stats(from_date: date, to_date: date, staff: StaffContext, by: str | None = None) -> dict:
    # ...기존 reserved/cancelled/no_show/visited/avg_wait/app_ratio/visits_by_hour 계산은 그대로 유지...

    breakdown: list[dict] = []
    if by in ("department", "doctor"):
        group_col = "a.department_id" if by == "department" else "a.doctor_id"
        async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
            breakdown_rows = await conn.fetch(
                f"""
                select {group_col} as group_id,
                       count(distinct h.appointment_id) filter (where h.to_status = '진료완료') as visited_by_event_date,
                       count(distinct h.appointment_id) filter (where h.to_status in ('환자취소', '병원취소')) as cancelled_by_event_date
                from appointments a
                join appointment_status_history h
                    on h.appointment_id = a.id and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
                where h.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
                group by {group_col}
                """,
                from_date, to_date,
            )
        breakdown = [
            {("department_id" if by == "department" else "doctor_id"): row["group_id"],
             "visited_by_event_date": row["visited_by_event_date"],
             "cancelled_by_event_date": row["cancelled_by_event_date"]}
            for row in breakdown_rows
        ]

    return {
        # ...기존 필드 그대로...
        "breakdown": breakdown,  # [2026-07-28 재검증] by가 없으면 빈 리스트
    }
```

```python
_STATS_DETAIL_STATUS = {
    "visited": ("진료완료",),
    "cancelled": ("환자취소", "병원취소"),
    "no_show": ("예약부도",),
}


async def get_stats_detail(metric: str, from_date: date, to_date: date, staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    # [2026-07-28 재검증] "예약(reserved)"은 상태전이가 아니라 생성 자체가 사건이라 별도 분기로 처리한다.
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        if metric == "reserved":
            rows = await conn.fetch(
                """
                select a.id, p.name as patient_name, s.slot_date, a.status
                from appointments a
                join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
                left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
                where a.created_at::date between $1 and $2
                order by a.created_at desc
                """,
                from_date, to_date,
            )
        else:
            statuses = _STATS_DETAIL_STATUS.get(metric)
            if not statuses:
                raise ValueError(f"알 수 없는 지표: {metric}")
            rows = await conn.fetch(
                """
                select distinct a.id, p.name as patient_name, s.slot_date, a.status
                from appointment_status_history h
                join appointments a on a.id = h.appointment_id
                join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
                left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
                where h.to_status = any($3::text[])
                  and h.from_status is distinct from h.to_status
                  and h.changed_at::date between $1 and $2
                """,
                from_date, to_date, list(statuses),
            )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]
```

`backend/app/routers/dashboard.py`에 라우트 추가:

```python
    to: date,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> list[dict]:
```

주: FastAPI는 경로를 등록 순서로 매칭하지 않고 정적 경로(`/stats/detail`)를 동적 경로보다 우선 매칭하므로, `/stats/{...}` 형태의 다른 라우트가 이후 추가되더라도 `/stats/detail`이 먼저 정의돼 있으면 안전하다. 다만 이 태스크에는 그런 충돌 라우트가 없다.

```typescript
import { renderHook } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { useRealtimeSubscription } from "./useRealtimeSubscription";

const mockChannel = {
  on: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),
  subscribe: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),
};

vi.mock("./supabaseClient", () => ({
  supabase: {
    channel: vi.fn(() => mockChannel),
    removeChannel: vi.fn(),
  },
}));

describe("useRealtimeSubscription", () => {
  it("subscribes to postgres_changes for the given table", () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    renderHook(() => useRealtimeSubscription("appointments", onChange));

    expect(mockChannel.on).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "postgres_changes",
      expect.objectContaining({ event: "*", schema: "public", table: "appointments" }),
      onChange,
    );
    expect(mockChannel.subscribe).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: useRealtimeSubscription 구현**

`frontend/src/lib/useRealtimeSubscription.ts`:
```typescript
import { useEffect } from "react";

import { supabase } from "./supabaseClient";

export function useRealtimeSubscription(table: string, onChange: () => void) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const channel = supabase
      .channel(`realtime-${table}`)
      .on("postgres_changes", { event: "*", schema: "public", table }, onChange)
      .subscribe();

    return () => {
      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
    };
  }, [table, onChange]);
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { HttpResponse, http } from "msw";

}));

function renderWithClient(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(<QueryClientProvider client={client}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
}

describe("PhoneBookingModal", () => {
  it("confirms identity twice — once when selecting the patient, once before final save — as required by the design spec (섹션 6: 검색 선택 시점 1회 + 예약 확정 시점 1회)", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/patients/find", () => HttpResponse.json({ patient_id: "p1" })),

      ),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/appointments", () => HttpResponse.json({ appointment_id: "a1" })),
    );
    const onBooked = vi.fn();

    renderWithClient(
      <PhoneBookingModal
        slotId="slot1"

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("생년월일"), "1985-03-01");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "환자 조회" }));

    // 1차 확인: 검색 결과에서 환자를 선택하는 시점
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(/홍길동/)).toBeInTheDocument());
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("방문 이유"), "감기");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "예약 저장" }));

    // 2차 확인: 예약을 최종 확정하는 시점 — 아직 서버 호출이 일어나면 안 됨
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(/이 내용으로 예약을 확정합니다/)).toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(onBooked).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(onBooked).toHaveBeenCalledWith("a1"));
  });

  it("cancelling the second confirmation returns to the reason input without booking", async () => {
    server.use(

        HttpResponse.json({ id: "p1", name: "홍길동", birth_date: "1985-03-01", phone: "01012345678", gender: "M", internal_notes: [] }),
      ),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/appointments", () => HttpResponse.json({ appointment_id: "a1" })),
    );
    const onBooked = vi.fn();

    renderWithClient(
      <PhoneBookingModal
        slotId="slot1"
        doctorId="doc1"
        departmentId="dept1"

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("전화번호"), "01012345678");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("생년월일"), "1985-03-01");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "환자 조회" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(/홍길동/)).toBeInTheDocument());
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "확인" }));
```

- [ ] **Step 5: PhoneBookingModal 구현**

`frontend/src/features/calendar/PhoneBookingModal.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";

import { createAppointment } from "../../api/appointments";
import { findPatient } from "../../api/patients";
import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";
import { ConfirmIdentityDialog } from "../../components/ConfirmIdentityDialog";

type Props = {
  slotId: string;
  doctorId: string;
  departmentId: string;
  onBooked: (appointmentId: string) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

type FoundPatient = { id: string; name: string; birth_date: string };

export function PhoneBookingModal({ slotId, doctorId, departmentId, onBooked, onCancel }: Props) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [birthDate, setBirthDate] = useState("");
  const [foundPatient, setFoundPatient] = useState<FoundPatient | null>(null);
  const [confirmedPatientId, setConfirmedPatientId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [showFinalConfirm, setShowFinalConfirm] = useState(false);

  async function handleSearch() {
    const result = await findPatient(phone, birthDate);
    if (!result.patient_id) {
      setFoundPatient(null);
      return;
    }
    const detail = await apiFetch<{ id: string; name: string; birth_date: string }>(
      `/patients/${result.patient_id}`,
    );
    setFoundPatient({ id: detail.id, name: detail.name, birth_date: detail.birth_date });
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!confirmedPatientId) return;
    const result = await createAppointment({
      account_patient_id: confirmedPatientId,
      for_patient_id: confirmedPatientId,
      department_id: departmentId,
      doctor_id: doctorId,
      reason,
      source: "staff",
      initial_status: "예약확정",
      slot_id: slotId,
    });
    setShowFinalConfirm(false);
    onBooked(result.appointment_id);
  }

  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="전화 예약 등록">
      <label htmlFor="phone-input">전화번호</label>
      <input id="phone-input" value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} />
      <label htmlFor="birth-date-input">생년월일</label>
      <input id="birth-date-input" value={birthDate} onChange={(e) => setBirthDate(e.target.value)} />
      <button type="button" onClick={handleSearch}>
        환자 조회
      </button>

      {/* 1차 동명이인 확인: 검색 결과에서 환자를 선택하는 시점 (설계서 섹션 6) */}
      {foundPatient && !confirmedPatientId && (
        <ConfirmIdentityDialog
          patientName={foundPatient.name}
          patientBirthDate={foundPatient.birth_date}
          actionLabel="환자로 선택합니다"
          onConfirm={() => setConfirmedPatientId(foundPatient.id)}
          onCancel={() => setFoundPatient(null)}
        />
      )}

      {confirmedPatientId && !showFinalConfirm && (
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="reason-input">방문 이유</label>
          <input id="reason-input" value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} />
          <button type="button" onClick={onCancel}>
            취소
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowFinalConfirm(true)}>
            예약 저장
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 2차 동명이인 확인: 예약을 최종 확정하는 시점 (설계서 섹션 6 — "검색 선택 시점 1회 + 확정 시점 1회") */}
      {showFinalConfirm && foundPatient && (
        <ConfirmIdentityDialog
          patientName={foundPatient.name}
          patientBirthDate={foundPatient.birth_date}
          actionLabel="이 내용으로 예약을 확정합니다"
          onConfirm={handleSave}
          onCancel={() => setShowFinalConfirm(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/features/calendar/CalendarPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import dayGridPlugin from "@fullcalendar/daygrid";
import interactionPlugin, { type EventClickArg } from "@fullcalendar/interaction";
import FullCalendar from "@fullcalendar/react";
import timeGridPlugin from "@fullcalendar/timegrid";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";
import { PhoneBookingModal } from "./PhoneBookingModal";

type Slot = {
  id: string;
  doctor_id: string;
  department_id: string;
  slot_date: string;
  start_time: string;
  status: "빈시간" | "예약됨" | "휴진";
  patient_name: string | null;
  has_schedule_conflict: boolean;
};

export function CalendarPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: slots } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["calendar-slots"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<Slot[]>("/calendar-slots"),
  });
  const [bookingSlot, setBookingSlot] = useState<Slot | null>(null);

  if (!slots) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  const events = slots.map((slot) => ({
    id: slot.id,
    title: slot.status === "예약됨" ? (slot.patient_name ?? "예약됨") : slot.status,
    date: `${slot.slot_date}T${slot.start_time}`,
    classNames: [
      slot.status === "빈시간" ? "slot-empty" : slot.status === "휴진" ? "slot-closed" : "slot-booked",
      slot.has_schedule_conflict ? "slot-conflict" : "",
    ],
  }));

  return (
    <div>
      <FullCalendar
        plugins={[dayGridPlugin, timeGridPlugin, interactionPlugin]}
        initialView="timeGridWeek"
        events={events}
        // 이전 버전은 dateClick(DateClickArg)에서 arg.event를 읽으려 했는데, dateClick의 인자에는
        // event 속성 자체가 없어(빈 칸 클릭 이벤트이지 슬롯 이벤트가 아님) 슬롯을 절대 찾지 못했다.
        // 슬롯은 이미 이벤트로 렌더링돼 있으므로 eventClick(EventClickArg)으로 식별한다.
        eventClick={(arg: EventClickArg) => {
          const slot = slots.find((s) => s.id === arg.event.id);
          if (slot && slot.status === "빈시간") setBookingSlot(slot);
        }}
      />
      {bookingSlot && (
        <PhoneBookingModal
          slotId={bookingSlot.id}
          doctorId={bookingSlot.doctor_id}
          departmentId={bookingSlot.department_id}
          onCancel={() => setBookingSlot(null)}
          onBooked={() => {
            setBookingSlot(null);
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["calendar-slots"] });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`/calendar-slots` 조회 엔드포인트(진료과·의사 필터 포함)는 이 태스크가 아니라 Task 17(백엔드 — 일정 CRUD + 캘린더 조회 API)에서 구현한다. 이 태스크의 핵심 산출물은 실시간 동기화 훅과 전화예약 등록 모달의 동명이인 확인 흐름이며, 둘 다 테스트로 검증된다.

## Task 15: 관리자 — 환자정보 열람 기록 조회 화면 (`/admin/access-logs`)

요구사항 3.1 마지막 항목: "누가 환자정보와 진료기록을 열어봤는지 관리자가 확인할 수 있어야 합니다." 1단계에서 `access_audit_log` 테이블과 기록 저장 로직(`audit_service.log_access`), 관리자 읽기용 RLS 정책(`admin_can_read_audit_log`)까지는 이미 만들어졌지만, 그 기록을 실제로 보여주는 화면은 1·2단계 어디에도 없었다. 이 태스크가 그 화면을 추가한다.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/services/audit_log_query_service.py`
- Create: `backend/app/routers/access_logs.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`
- Create: `frontend/src/api/accessLogs.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/admin/AccessLogPage.tsx`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_audit_log_query_service.py`
- Test: `frontend/src/features/admin/AccessLogPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.db.pool.acquire_as`(1단계 Task 9), `app.core.security.StaffContext, require_role`, DB 테이블 `access_audit_log`(1단계 Task 8, `admin_can_read_audit_log` RLS 정책 재사용)
- Produces: `GET /admin/access-logs?patient_id=`(관리자 전용), `app.services.audit_log_query_service.list_access_logs(staff: StaffContext, patient_id: UUID | None = None) -> list[dict]`
- Produces: `<AccessLogPage />` — `/admin/access-logs` 라우트(관리자 전용, `<RequireRole roles={["admin"]}>`로 감싸 등록)

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성 — audit_log_query_service**

`backend/tests/test_audit_log_query_service.py`:
```python
import pytest

from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.services import audit_log_query_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str) -> StaffContext:
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=None)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_access_logs_returns_staff_and_patient_names_newest_first(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    receptionist = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type, accessed_at)
        values ($1, $2, 'patient_detail', now() - interval '1 hour')
        """,
        receptionist["staff_id"], patient_id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type, accessed_at)
        values ($1, $2, 'medical_record', now())
        """,
        receptionist["staff_id"], patient_id,
    )

    logs = await audit_log_query_service.list_access_logs(admin)

    assert len(logs) == 2
    assert logs[0]["resource_type"] == "medical_record"
    assert logs[0]["patient_name"] == "홍길동"
    assert logs[0]["staff_name"] == "Test Staff"

    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type) values ($1, $2, 'patient_detail')",
        receptionist["staff_id"], patient_a,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type) values ($1, $2, 'patient_detail')",
        receptionist["staff_id"], patient_b,
    )

    logs = await audit_log_query_service.list_access_logs(admin, patient_id=patient_a)

    assert len(logs) == 1
    assert logs[0]["patient_name"] == "홍길동"
```

```python
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as


async def list_access_logs(staff: StaffContext, patient_id: UUID | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select a.id, a.resource_type, a.accessed_at,
                   s.name as staff_name, p.name as patient_name
            from access_audit_log a
            join staff s on s.id = a.staff_id
            join patients p on p.id = a.patient_id
            where ($1::uuid is null or a.patient_id = $1)
            order by a.accessed_at desc
            limit 200
            """,
            patient_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]
```

`backend/app/routers/access_logs.py`:
```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import audit_log_query_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["access-logs"])


@router.get("/admin/access-logs")
async def access_logs(
    patient_id: UUID | None = None,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> list[dict]:
    return await audit_log_query_service.list_access_logs(staff, patient_id)
```

`frontend/src/features/admin/AccessLogPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { listAccessLogs } from "../../api/accessLogs";

const RESOURCE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  patient_detail: "환자정보",
  medical_record: "진료기록",
};

export function AccessLogPage() {
  const { data } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["access-logs"], queryFn: listAccessLogs });

  if (!data) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  return (
    <table>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>열람 시각</th>
          <th>열람 직원</th>
          <th>환자</th>
          <th>열람 자료</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {data.map((log) => (
          <tr key={log.id}>
            <td>{log.accessed_at}</td>
            <td>{log.staff_name}</td>
            <td>{log.patient_name}</td>
            <td>{RESOURCE_LABEL[log.resource_type] ?? log.resource_type}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

## Task 16: 취소요청 대기열 (`/cancellation-requests`) — 스펙 섹션 10

3단계(환자 앱)가 마감 후 취소 요청 시 채워두는 `appointments.cancellation_requested_at`을 직원이 승인/반려하는 화면. 요구사항 4.3 "병원이 정한 시간 이후에는 앱에서 직접 취소하지 못하고 상담으로 연결"을 완결하기 위해 3단계 스펙이 2단계 몫으로 남겨뒀던 항목(스펙 섹션 10 참고).

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/services/appointment_service.py` (`list_cancellation_requests`, `approve_cancellation_request`, `reject_cancellation_request` 추가)
- Modify: `backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py` (`get_today_summary`에 `cancellation_requests_count` 추가)
- Create: `backend/app/routers/cancellation_requests.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/today/useTodaySummary.ts`
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/today/TodayPage.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/today/TodayPage.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/api/cancellationRequests.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/cancellationRequests/CancellationRequestsPage.tsx`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_appointment_service.py` (추가)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_dashboard_service.py` (추가)
- Test: `frontend/src/features/cancellationRequests/CancellationRequestsPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.db.pool.acquire_as`(1단계 Task 9), `app.core.errors.AppError`, `app.core.security.StaffContext, require_role`(1단계 Task 9), `app.services.slot_service.release_slot`(3단계 Task 7), `app.services.notification_service.notify_patient`(3단계 Task 8, `"cancellation_rejected"` 메시지는 이미 등록되어 있음), `appointments.cancellation_requested_at`(3단계 Task 2)
- Produces: `GET /cancellation-requests`, `POST /cancellation-requests/{id}/approve`, `POST /cancellation-requests/{id}/reject` (모두 `require_role("receptionist", "admin")`)
- Produces: `app.services.appointment_service.list_cancellation_requests(staff) -> list[dict]`(각 행 `masked_birth_date` 포함, 원본 `birth_date` 미포함 — [정합성 검토 R5-06]), `approve_cancellation_request(appointment_id, staff) -> None`, `reject_cancellation_request(appointment_id, staff) -> None`
- Produces: `<CancellationRequestsPage />` — `/cancellation-requests` 라우트

```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_cancellation_requests_returns_only_requested(db_conn):
    ctx = await _seed_base(db_conn)
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time) values ($1, '2026-08-01', '09:00') returning id",
        ctx["doctor"].id,
    )
    requested_id = await appointment_service.create_appointment(
        staff=ctx["receptionist"], account_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"], for_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"],
        department_id=ctx["dept_id"], doctor_id=ctx["doctor"].id, reason="감기",
        source="app", initial_status="예약확정", slot_id=slot_id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        "update appointments set cancellation_requested_at = now() where id = $1", requested_id,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into appointment_status_history (appointment_id, from_status, to_status, changed_by_patient_id, reason)
        values ($1, '예약확정', '예약확정', $2, '급한 사정')
        """,
        requested_id, ctx["patient_id"],
    )
    await appointment_service.create_appointment(
        staff=ctx["receptionist"], account_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"], for_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"],
        department_id=ctx["dept_id"], doctor_id=ctx["doctor"].id, reason="두통",
        source="app", initial_status="예약확정",
    )

    rows = await appointment_service.list_cancellation_requests(ctx["admin"])

    assert len(rows) == 1
    assert rows[0]["id"] == requested_id
    assert rows[0]["reason"] == "급한 사정"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: appointment_service 구현**

`backend/app/services/appointment_service.py` 상단 import를 다음으로 교체(기존 `from app.services.slot_service import book_slot`를 확장하고 `notification_service`/`mask_birth_date` 추가):
```python
from app.core.masking import mask_birth_date  # [정합성 검토 R5-06]
from app.services import notification_service
from app.services.slot_service import book_slot, release_slot
```

파일 끝에 추가:
```python
async def list_cancellation_requests(staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select a.id, p.name as patient_name, p.birth_date as raw_birth_date,
                   s.slot_date, s.start_time,
                   d.name as department_name, doc.name as doctor_name,
                   a.cancellation_requested_at,
                   (
                       select h.reason from appointment_status_history h
                       where h.appointment_id = a.id and h.reason is not null
                       order by h.changed_at desc limit 1
                   ) as reason
            from appointments a
            join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
            left join appointment_slots s on s.id = a.slot_id
            join departments d on d.id = a.department_id
            join staff doc on doc.id = a.doctor_id
            where a.cancellation_requested_at is not null
            order by a.cancellation_requested_at
            """
        )
    # [정합성 검토 R5-06] 목록 응답은 masked_birth_date만 담는다.
    return [
        {**{k: v for k, v in dict(row).items() if k != "raw_birth_date"}, "masked_birth_date": mask_birth_date(row["raw_birth_date"])}
        for row in rows
    ]


async def approve_cancellation_request(appointment_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> None:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select status, slot_id, for_patient_id, account_patient_id, cancellation_requested_at "
            "from appointments where id = $1",
            appointment_id,
        )
        if row is None or row["cancellation_requested_at"] is None:
            raise AppError("취소 요청을 찾을 수 없습니다. 이미 처리되었을 수 있습니다.", status_code=404)

        # 이력(appointment_status_history)은 1단계 log_appointment_status_change() 트리거가 자동으로 남긴다 —
        # 직접 INSERT는 금지돼 있으므로(치명적 규칙은 DB가 최종 심판), 사유만 세션에 실어 넘긴다.
        await conn.execute("select set_config('app.status_change_reason', '취소 요청 승인', true)")
        # [정합성 검토 R3-02] 취소로 끝나는 예약은 더 이상 "처리 필요" 대상이 아니므로 플래그도 정리한다
        # (오늘현황 카드는 이미 상태 필터로 취소 건을 세지 않지만, 데이터 위생 차원에서 명시적으로 끈다).
        await conn.execute(
            "update appointments set status = '환자취소', cancellation_requested_at = null, "
            "updated_at = now(), needs_rescheduling = false where id = $1",
            appointment_id,
        )
        if row["slot_id"] is not None:
            await release_slot(row["slot_id"], staff, conn=conn)

        account_patient_id = row["account_patient_id"]
        target_name = None
        if row["for_patient_id"] != account_patient_id:
            target_name = await conn.fetchval("select name from patients where id = $1", row["for_patient_id"])

    # [정합성 검토 R2-05] 취소요청 승인은 기존 MESSAGES에 없던 cancellation_approved를 새로 쓴다.
    try:
        await notification_service.notify_patient(
            account_patient_id, "cancellation_approved", target_name=target_name, appointment_id=appointment_id,
        )
    except Exception:
        pass


async def reject_cancellation_request(appointment_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> None:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        # [정합성 검토 R2-05] 이전 버전은 진료 대상 환자(for_patient_id)를 그대로 수신자로 썼다.
        # 가족 예약(계정 소유자가 실제 진료받는 사람과 다른 경우)에는 계정 소유자에게 보내야
        # 알림을 확인할 수 있으므로 account_patient_id를 수신자로 쓰고, appointment_id도 함께
        # 넘겨 다른 알림과 같은 중복 방지 규칙을 적용한다.
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select status, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, cancellation_requested_at "
            "from appointments where id = $1",
            appointment_id,
        )
        if row is None or row["cancellation_requested_at"] is None:
            raise AppError("취소 요청을 찾을 수 없습니다. 이미 처리되었을 수 있습니다.", status_code=404)

        await conn.execute(
            "update appointments set cancellation_requested_at = null, updated_at = now() where id = $1",
            appointment_id,
        )
        await conn.execute(
            """
            insert into appointment_status_history (appointment_id, from_status, to_status, changed_by, reason)
            values ($1, $2, $2, $3, '취소 요청 반려')
            """,
            appointment_id, row["status"], staff.id,
        )
        account_patient_id = row["account_patient_id"]
        target_name = None
        if row["for_patient_id"] != account_patient_id:
            target_name = await conn.fetchval("select name from patients where id = $1", row["for_patient_id"])

    try:
        await notification_service.notify_patient(
            account_patient_id, "cancellation_rejected", target_name=target_name, appointment_id=appointment_id,
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
```

```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import appointment_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["cancellation-requests"])


@router.get("/cancellation-requests")
async def list_cancellation_requests(
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> list[dict]:
    return await appointment_service.list_cancellation_requests(staff)


@router.post("/cancellation-requests/{appointment_id}/approve")
async def approve_cancellation_request(
    appointment_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> dict:
    await appointment_service.approve_cancellation_request(appointment_id, staff)
    return {"ok": True}


@router.post("/cancellation-requests/{appointment_id}/reject")
async def reject_cancellation_request(
    appointment_id: UUID,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> dict:
    await appointment_service.reject_cancellation_request(appointment_id, staff)
    return {"ok": True}
```

```typescript
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { apiFetch } from "../../api/httpClient";

export type TodaySummary = {
  long_wait_patients: { appointment_id: string; patient_name: string }[];
  urgent_patients: { appointment_id: string; patient_name: string }[];
  pending_inquiries_count: number;
  affected_appointments_count: number;
  cancellation_requests_count: number;
  total_reserved: number;
  arrived: number;
  waiting: number;
  in_progress: number;
  completed: number;
  cancelled_or_no_show: number;
};
```

`frontend/src/features/today/TodayPage.tsx`를 다음으로 교체(`cancellation_requests_count` 조건과 카드 추가, 링크 클릭 시 `/cancellation-requests`로 이동):
```tsx
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import { StatTile } from "../../components/StatTile";
import { useTodaySummary } from "./useTodaySummary";

export function TodayPage() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useTodaySummary();

  if (isLoading || !data) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  const hasActionItems =
    data.long_wait_patients.length > 0 ||
    data.urgent_patients.length > 0 ||
    data.pending_inquiries_count > 0 ||
    data.affected_appointments_count > 0 ||
    data.cancellation_requests_count > 0;

  return (
    <div>
      {hasActionItems && (
        <section aria-label="지금 처리할 것">
          {data.long_wait_patients.map((p) => (
            <p key={p.appointment_id}>오래 대기: {p.patient_name}</p>
          ))}
          {data.urgent_patients.map((p) => (
            <p key={p.appointment_id}>응급/주의: {p.patient_name}</p>
          ))}
          {data.pending_inquiries_count > 0 && <p>확인 필요 상담 문의: {data.pending_inquiries_count}건</p>}
          {data.affected_appointments_count > 0 && (
            <p>일정변경으로 확인 필요한 예약: {data.affected_appointments_count}건</p>
          )}
          {data.cancellation_requests_count > 0 && (
            <p>
              <Link to="/cancellation-requests">취소 요청 확인 {data.cancellation_requests_count}건</Link>
            </p>
          )}
        </section>
      )}
      <section aria-label="오늘 요약">
        <StatTile label="전체 예약" value={data.total_reserved} />
        <StatTile label="도착" value={data.arrived} />
        <StatTile label="대기" value={data.waiting} />
        <StatTile label="진료중" value={data.in_progress} />
        <StatTile label="완료" value={data.completed} />
        <StatTile label="취소/부도" value={data.cancelled_or_no_show} />
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9.5: [2026-07-28 재검증] 오늘현황 `StatTile` 클릭 시 관련 목록으로 이동**

1차 정합성 검토 우선5가 지적한 대로, `StatTile`은 Task 7에서 이미 `onClick?: () => void`를 받도록 만들어져 있었지만(라인 1636 부근) `TodayPage`가 실제로 넘겨준 적이 없어 타일을 클릭해도 아무 반응이 없었다. `/queue`(Task 9의 `QueuePage`, `get_queue_today` 기반)는 `'도착', '진료대기', '진료중'` 상태만 보여준다(Task 13 `get_queue_today` 쿼리 참고) — 그래서 그 세 상태에 정확히 대응하는 "도착"·"대기"·"진료중"과, 그 상위 개념인 "전체 예약" 타일만 `/queue`로 이동시킨다. "완료"와 "취소/부도"는 `/queue`에 나타나지 않는 상태이고 이 계획서에는 그 상태만 걸러 보여주는 목록 화면이 아직 없으므로, 이번에는 `onClick`을 주지 않고(눌러도 반응 없음이 아니라 애초에 클릭 가능한 타일로 렌더링하지 않음 — `StatTile`은 `onClick`이 없으면 버튼이 아닌 일반 텍스트로 렌더링된다, Task 7 Step 5 참고) 향후 "완료/취소 목록" 화면이 생기면 연결하기로 남겨둔다.

- [ ] **Step 11: cancellationRequests API 클라이언트 + CancellationRequestsPage 구현**

`frontend/src/api/cancellationRequests.ts`:
```typescript
import { apiFetch } from "./httpClient";

export type CancellationRequest = {
  id: string;
  patient_name: string;
  masked_birth_date: string;
  slot_date: string | null;
  start_time: string | null;
  department_name: string;
  doctor_name: string;
  cancellation_requested_at: string;
  reason: string | null;
};

export function listCancellationRequests() {
  return apiFetch<CancellationRequest[]>("/cancellation-requests");
}

export function approveCancellationRequest(appointmentId: string) {
  return apiFetch<{ ok: boolean }>(`/cancellation-requests/${appointmentId}/approve`, { method: "POST" });
}

export function rejectCancellationRequest(appointmentId: string) {
  return apiFetch<{ ok: boolean }>(`/cancellation-requests/${appointmentId}/reject`, { method: "POST" });
}
```

`frontend/src/features/cancellationRequests/CancellationRequestsPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import {
  approveCancellationRequest,
  listCancellationRequests,
  rejectCancellationRequest,
} from "../../api/cancellationRequests";

export function CancellationRequestsPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["cancellation-requests"], queryFn: listCancellationRequests });

  async function handleApprove(id: string) {
    if (!window.confirm("이 취소 요청을 승인할까요? 예약이 취소 처리됩니다.")) return;
    await approveCancellationRequest(id);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["cancellation-requests"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["today-summary"] });
  }

  async function handleReject(id: string) {
    if (!window.confirm("이 취소 요청을 반려할까요? 예약은 그대로 유지됩니다.")) return;
    await rejectCancellationRequest(id);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["cancellation-requests"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["today-summary"] });
  }

  if (!data) return <p>불러오는 중입니다...</p>;

  return (
    <table>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>환자</th>
          <th>예약 일시</th>
          <th>진료과/의사</th>
          <th>요청 시각</th>
          <th>사유</th>
          <th>처리</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {data.map((r) => (
          <tr key={r.id}>
            <td>{r.patient_name}({r.masked_birth_date})</td>
            <td>{r.slot_date ? `${r.slot_date} ${r.start_time}` : "워크인"}</td>
            <td>{r.department_name}/{r.doctor_name}</td>
            <td>{r.cancellation_requested_at}</td>
            <td>{r.reason ?? "-"}</td>
            <td>
              <button onClick={() => handleApprove(r.id)}>승인</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleReject(r.id)}>반려</button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```


---

## Task 17: 진료과/의사 일정관리 백엔드 (CRUD + 캘린더 조회) — Task 12에서 분리·승격

> 정합성 검토에서 확인된 갭: 일정 CRUD, `/calendar-slots` 조회 API가 계획에 없어 SchedulePage(Task 12→18)가 실제로 저장할 API가 존재하지 않았다. `doctor_schedule_rules`/`doctor_schedule_exceptions` 테이블과 RLS는 1단계 Task 3에 이미 있으므로, 여기서는 그 위에 서비스·라우터만 추가한다.
>
> **[정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02 추가]** 위 CRUD는 규칙·예외를 저장할 뿐, 실제 `appointment_slots` 행을 만들지 않는다. 그래서 `max_daily_appointments`·점심시간·휴진일이 실제 예약 가능 여부에 반영되지 않는 문제가 있었다. 이번에 `regenerate_slots`(슬롯 생성기)를 추가해 규칙 저장 시 앞으로 8주치 슬롯을 실제로 재계산하고, `preview_rule_change`로 저장 전 영향받는 예약을 미리 보여준다.
>
> **[정합성 검토 R3-03 추가]** `appointments`/`appointment_slots`/`appointment_status_history`가 `supabase_realtime` publication에 등록되어 있지 않아, 코드가 Realtime을 구독해도 실제 원격 Supabase 환경에서는 변경사항이 전달되지 않는 문제가 있었다(현재 publication에는 4단계 챗봇 테이블만 등록됨). Step 2 마이그레이션에서 이 그룹의 슬롯 컬럼 추가와 함께 등록한다.

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/00101_appointments_needs_rescheduling_and_realtime.sql`
- Modify: `backend/app/services/schedule_service.py` (Task 2에서 만든 `list_affected_appointments`에 CRUD 함수 추가)
- Modify: `backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py` (Task 13의 `get_today_summary` "처리 필요" 카드 연동)
- Modify: `backend/app/services/appointment_service.py` (Task 2의 `reschedule_appointment`에 `needs_rescheduling` 해제 추가)
- Create: `backend/app/routers/schedule.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_schedule_service.py` (추가)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_dashboard_service.py` (추가)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.db.pool.acquire_as`, `app.core.security.StaffContext, require_role`(1단계), `app.services.appointment_service.reschedule_appointment`(Task 2), `list_affected_appointments`(Task 2)
- Produces: `app.services.schedule_service.list_departments() -> list[dict]`, `create_department(name, staff) -> UUID`, `list_schedule_rules(doctor_id) -> list[dict]`, `upsert_schedule_rule(doctor_id, weekday, rule, staff) -> UUID`, `list_schedule_exceptions(doctor_id) -> list[dict]`, `create_schedule_exception(doctor_id, exception_date, is_closed, override_start_time, override_end_time, notify_appointment_ids, staff) -> UUID`, `list_calendar_slots(department_id, doctor_id, from_date, to_date, staff) -> list[dict]`, `regenerate_slots(doctor_id, staff, dry_run=False) -> dict`([정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] `{"affected_appointments": list[dict], "slots_added": int, "slots_removed": int}`), `preview_rule_change(doctor_id, weekday, rule, staff) -> list[dict]`([정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 저장하지 않고 영향받는 예약만 미리 계산)
- Produces: `GET /departments`, `POST /departments`, `GET /doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-rules`, `PUT /doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-rules/{weekday}`, `POST /doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-rules/{weekday}/preview`, `GET /doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-exceptions`, `POST /doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-exceptions`, `GET /calendar-slots?department_id=&doctor_id=&from=&to=`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`backend/tests/test_schedule_service.py`에 추가(파일이 없으면 새로 작성):
```python
import pytest

from app.core.errors import AppError
from app.services import schedule_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str, department_id=None):
    from app.core.security import StaffContext
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=department_id)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_admin_can_create_department(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    dept_id = await schedule_service.create_department("내과", admin)
    departments = await schedule_service.list_departments()
    assert any(d["id"] == dept_id and d["name"] == "내과" for d in departments)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_non_admin_cannot_create_department(db_conn):
    receptionist = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist"), "receptionist")
    with pytest.raises(AppError):
        await schedule_service.create_department("내과", receptionist)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_upsert_schedule_rule_creates_then_updates(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")

    rule_id = await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=1,
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "18:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 20,
              "lunch_start": "12:00", "lunch_end": "13:00", "max_daily_appointments": 20, "booking_deadline": "17:00"},
        staff=admin,
    )
    rules = await schedule_service.list_schedule_rules(doctor["staff_id"])
    assert any(r["id"] == rule_id and r["max_daily_appointments"] == 20 for r in rules)

    rule_id_2 = await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=1,
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "17:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 20,
              "lunch_start": "12:00", "lunch_end": "13:00", "max_daily_appointments": 15, "booking_deadline": "16:00"},
        staff=admin,
    )
    assert rule_id_2 == rule_id  # 같은 요일은 갱신(upsert), 신규 행을 만들지 않는다
    rules = await schedule_service.list_schedule_rules(doctor["staff_id"])
    assert len(rules) == 1
    assert rules[0]["max_daily_appointments"] == 15


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_doctor_schedule_rules_unique_per_weekday(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-01] (doctor_id, weekday) UNIQUE 제약이 실제로 걸려 있는지 직접 확인한다.
    이 제약이 upsert_schedule_rule의 INSERT ... ON CONFLICT를 동시 요청에도 안전하게 만든다 —
    제약이 없으면 ON CONFLICT가 걸 대상이 없어 그냥 일반 INSERT처럼 동작해 중복 행이 생긴다."""
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into doctor_schedule_rules
            (doctor_id, weekday, start_time, end_time, slot_duration_minutes, max_daily_appointments)
        values ($1, 1, '09:00', '18:00', 20, 20)
        """,
        doctor["staff_id"],
    )
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        await db_conn.execute(
            """
            insert into doctor_schedule_rules
                (doctor_id, weekday, start_time, end_time, slot_duration_minutes, max_daily_appointments)
            values ($1, 1, '10:00', '19:00', 30, 10)
            """,
            doctor["staff_id"],
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_create_schedule_exception_notifies_selected_affected_appointments(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    receptionist = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist"), "receptionist")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    await db_conn.execute("update staff set department_id = $1 where id = $2", dept_id, doctor["staff_id"])
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time) values ($1, '2026-08-01', '09:00') returning id",
        doctor["staff_id"],
    )
    appointment_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        """
        insert into appointments (slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4, '예약확정', 'staff', $5)
        returning id
        """,
        slot_id, patient_id, dept_id, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist.id,
    )

    exception_id = await schedule_service.create_schedule_exception(
        doctor["staff_id"], exception_date="2026-08-01", is_closed=True,
        override_start_time=None, override_end_time=None,
        notify_appointment_ids=[appointment_id], staff=admin,
    )
    assert exception_id is not None

    row = await db_conn.fetchrow("select status from appointments where id = $1", appointment_id)
    assert row["status"] in ("병원취소", "예약확정")  # 안내대상으로 선택된 예약은 재조정/취소 처리 대상이 된다

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_calendar_slots_filters_by_department_and_doctor(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")

    await db_conn.execute("update staff set department_id = $1 where id = $2", dept_id, doctor_a["staff_id"])
    await db_conn.execute("update staff set department_id = $1 where id = $2", other_dept_id, doctor_b["staff_id"])
    await db_conn.execute(

    )

    )

    slots = await schedule_service.list_calendar_slots(

    )
    assert {s["doctor_id"] for s in slots} == {doctor_a["staff_id"]}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_regenerate_slots_respects_lunch_and_max_daily(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 슬롯 생성기는 점심시간을 제외하고 하루최대인원만큼만 슬롯을 만든다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    from datetime import date, timedelta
    target_weekday = (date.today() + timedelta(days=1)).weekday()
    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_weekday,
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "13:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": "12:00", "lunch_end": "13:00", "max_daily_appointments": 3, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )

    result = await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor["staff_id"], admin)

    assert result["affected_appointments"] == []
    target_date = date.today() + timedelta(days=1)
    while target_date.weekday() != target_weekday:
        target_date += timedelta(days=1)
    slots = await db_conn.fetch(
        "select start_time from appointment_slots where doctor_id = $1 and slot_date = $2 order by start_time",
        doctor["staff_id"], target_date,
    )
    # 09:00-12:00(점심 전)에서 30분 간격 6칸이 가능하지만 max_daily_appointments=3이라 앞의 3칸만 생성된다.
    assert [str(s["start_time"])[:5] for s in slots] == ["09:00", "09:30", "10:00"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_regenerate_slots_skips_closed_exception_day(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-02] 휴진 예외가 걸린 날짜는 슬롯을 만들지 않는다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")

    target_date = date.today() + timedelta(days=2)
    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "10:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": None, "lunch_end": None, "max_daily_appointments": 10, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into doctor_schedule_exceptions (doctor_id, exception_date, is_closed) values ($1, $2, true)",
        doctor["staff_id"], target_date,
    )

    await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor["staff_id"], admin)

    slots = await db_conn.fetch(
        "select id from appointment_slots where doctor_id = $1 and slot_date = $2", doctor["staff_id"], target_date,
    )
    assert slots == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_regenerate_slots_keeps_booked_slot_and_reports_affected(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 이미 예약된 슬롯은 새 규칙에서 빠져도 삭제하지 않고, 영향받는 예약으로 보고한다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    receptionist = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    await db_conn.execute("update staff set department_id = $1 where id = $2", dept_id, doctor["staff_id"])
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    from datetime import date, timedelta
    target_date = date.today() + timedelta(days=3)
    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "18:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": None, "lunch_end": None, "max_daily_appointments": 20, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "select id from appointment_slots where doctor_id = $1 and slot_date = $2 and start_time = '17:30'",
        doctor["staff_id"], target_date,
    )
    await db_conn.execute("update appointment_slots set status = '예약됨' where id = $1", slot_id)
    appointment_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        """
        insert into appointments (slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4, '예약확정', 'staff', $5)
        returning id
        """,
        slot_id, patient_id, dept_id, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist["staff_id"],
    )

    # 하루최대인원을 줄여 17:30 슬롯이 더는 규칙에 들지 않게 만든다.
    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "18:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": None, "lunch_end": None, "max_daily_appointments": 5, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    result = await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor["staff_id"], admin)

    assert len(result["affected_appointments"]) == 1
    assert result["affected_appointments"][0]["id"] == appointment_id
    still_exists = await db_conn.fetchval("select status from appointment_slots where id = $1", slot_id)
    assert still_exists == "예약됨"  # 삭제되지 않고 그대로 유지
```
