```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_preview_rule_change_does_not_persist(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 미리보기는 규칙을 저장하지 않고 영향받는 예약만 계산한다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    from datetime import date, timedelta
    target_date = date.today() + timedelta(days=4)
    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "10:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": None, "lunch_end": None, "max_daily_appointments": 10, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor["staff_id"], admin)

    affected = await schedule_service.preview_rule_change(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "10:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": "09:00", "lunch_end": "10:00", "max_daily_appointments": 10, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    assert affected == []  # 빈 슬롯만 영향받으므로(예약 없음) 영향받는 "예약"은 없다

    saved_rules = await schedule_service.list_schedule_rules(doctor["staff_id"])
    assert not any(r["lunch_start"] is not None for r in saved_rules)  # 미리보기가 실제 규칙을 바꾸지 않음
```

Run: `cd backend && pytest tests/test_schedule_service.py -v`
Expected: FAIL (신규 함수 없음)

- [ ] **Step 2: 마이그레이션 — needs_rescheduling 컬럼 + Realtime publication 등록**

`supabase/migrations/00101_appointments_needs_rescheduling_and_realtime.sql`:
```sql
-- [정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 슬롯 재계산 결과 새 규칙에서 빠진 시간대에 이미 예약이 있으면,
-- 예약 자체는 건드리지 않고 이 플래그만 세워 접수직원의 "처리 필요" 목록에 남긴다.
alter table appointments add column needs_rescheduling boolean not null default false;

-- [정합성 검토 R3-03] 예약 관련 테이블이 supabase_realtime publication에 없어서, 앱/직원 웹이
-- 구독 코드를 갖고 있어도 실제 원격 환경에서는 변경사항이 전달되지 않았다(챗봇 테이블만 등록되어 있었음).
-- RLS는 Realtime 구독 결과에도 그대로 적용되므로(Supabase 기본 동작), 별도 필터링 코드 없이도
-- 각 사용자는 자신이 SELECT 가능한 행의 변경만 받는다 — 배포 후 실제 환경에서 이 점을 검증한다(Step 6).
alter publication supabase_realtime add table appointments, appointment_slots, appointment_status_history;
```

Run: `supabase db reset`
Expected: 오류 없이 적용됨

- [ ] **Step 3: schedule_service에 CRUD·조회 함수 추가**

`backend/app/services/schedule_service.py`(Task 2에서 만든 `list_affected_appointments` 아래에 추가):
```python
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.errors import AppError
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as
from app.services import notification_service  # [정합성 검토 R2-05]
from app.services.appointment_service import reschedule_appointment


async def list_departments() -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(None) as conn:  # 조회는 활성 직원 전체에 허용된 RLS이므로 별도 컨텍스트 불필요
        rows = await conn.fetch("select id, name from departments order by name")
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def create_department(name: str, staff: StaffContext) -> UUID:
    if staff.role != "admin":
        raise AppError("진료과 관리는 관리자만 할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        return await conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ($1) returning id", name)


async def list_schedule_rules(doctor_id: UUID) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(None) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            "select * from doctor_schedule_rules where doctor_id = $1 order by weekday", doctor_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def upsert_schedule_rule(doctor_id: UUID, weekday: int, rule: dict, staff: StaffContext) -> UUID:
    """[정합성 검토 R3-01] 이전 버전은 SELECT로 존재를 확인한 뒤 결과에 따라 별도 UPDATE/INSERT를
    실행했다. 두 관리자(또는 같은 관리자의 이중 클릭)가 같은 의사·요일 규칙을 동시에 처음 저장하면
    둘 다 SELECT에서 "없음"을 보고 각자 INSERT해 중복 행이 생길 수 있었다. `doctor_schedule_rules`에
    (doctor_id, weekday) UNIQUE 제약을 추가하고, `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` 하나로 원자화해
    이제는 동시 요청이 와도 항상 정확히 1행만 남는다."""
    if staff.role != "admin":
        raise AppError("일정 관리는 관리자만 할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        return await conn.fetchval(
            """
            insert into doctor_schedule_rules
                (doctor_id, weekday, start_time, end_time, slot_duration_minutes, lunch_start, lunch_end, max_daily_appointments, booking_deadline)
            values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9)
            on conflict (doctor_id, weekday) do update
                set start_time = excluded.start_time, end_time = excluded.end_time,
                    slot_duration_minutes = excluded.slot_duration_minutes,
                    lunch_start = excluded.lunch_start, lunch_end = excluded.lunch_end,
                    max_daily_appointments = excluded.max_daily_appointments,
                    booking_deadline = excluded.booking_deadline
            returning id
            """,
            doctor_id, weekday, rule["start_time"], rule["end_time"], rule["slot_duration_minutes"],
            rule.get("lunch_start"), rule.get("lunch_end"), rule["max_daily_appointments"], rule.get("booking_deadline"),
        )


async def list_schedule_exceptions(doctor_id: UUID) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(None) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            "select * from doctor_schedule_exceptions where doctor_id = $1 order by exception_date", doctor_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def create_schedule_exception(
    doctor_id: UUID,
    exception_date: str,
    is_closed: bool,
    override_start_time: str | None,
    override_end_time: str | None,
    notify_appointment_ids: list[UUID],
    staff: StaffContext,
) -> UUID:
    """휴진/시간변경 예외를 저장하고, 관리자가 안내대상으로 선택한 예약만 재조정 처리한다(AffectedAppointmentsDialog, Task 12/18)."""
    if staff.role != "admin":
        raise AppError("일정 예외 등록은 관리자만 할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        exception_id = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            insert into doctor_schedule_exceptions (doctor_id, exception_date, is_closed, override_start_time, override_end_time)
            values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
            on conflict (doctor_id, exception_date) do update
              set is_closed = excluded.is_closed,
                  override_start_time = excluded.override_start_time,
                  override_end_time = excluded.override_end_time
            returning id
            """,
            doctor_id, exception_date, is_closed, override_start_time, override_end_time,
        )
        # 안내대상으로 선택되지 않은 예약은 그대로 둔다 — 관리자가 개별 확인 후 선택한 예약만 처리한다.
        notify_targets = []  # [정합성 검토 R2-05] (account_patient_id, target_name) — 트랜잭션 밖에서 발송
        for appointment_id in notify_appointment_ids:
            row = await conn.fetchrow(
                "select slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id from appointments where id = $1", appointment_id,
            )
            if row is None:
                continue
            if is_closed:
                if row["slot_id"] is not None:
                    await conn.execute(
                        "update appointment_slots set status = '휴진' where id = $1", row["slot_id"],
                    )
                await conn.execute("select set_config('app.status_change_reason', '의사 일정 변경(휴진)', true)")
                # [정합성 검토 R3-02] 이 건은 관리자가 직접 확인하고 취소 처리를 선택한 예약이므로
                # (notify_appointment_ids로 골라 여기 도달), needs_rescheduling도 함께 정리한다.
                await conn.execute(
                    "update appointments set status = '병원취소', updated_at = now(), needs_rescheduling = false "
                    "where id = $1",
                    appointment_id,
                )
                target_name = None
                if row["for_patient_id"] != row["account_patient_id"]:
                    target_name = await conn.fetchval(
                        "select name from patients where id = $1", row["for_patient_id"],
                    )
                notify_targets.append((row["account_patient_id"], target_name, appointment_id))

    # [정합성 검토 R2-05] 병원측 취소 알림 — 상태 전이 트랜잭션이 커밋된 직후, best-effort로 호출한다.
    for account_patient_id, target_name, appt_id in notify_targets:
        try:
            await notification_service.notify_patient(
                account_patient_id, "hospital_cancelled", target_name=target_name, appointment_id=appt_id,
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
    return exception_id


async def list_calendar_slots(
    department_id: UUID | None, doctor_id: UUID | None, from_date: str, to_date: str, staff: StaffContext,
) -> list[dict]:
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select s.id, s.doctor_id, st.department_id, s.slot_date, s.start_time, s.status,
                   p.name as patient_name,
                   exists (
                     select 1 from doctor_schedule_exceptions e
                     where e.doctor_id = s.doctor_id and e.exception_date = s.slot_date
                   ) as has_schedule_conflict
            from appointment_slots s
            join staff st on st.id = s.doctor_id
            left join appointments a on a.slot_id = s.id
            left join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
            where s.slot_date between $1 and $2
              and ($3::uuid is null or st.department_id = $3)
              and ($4::uuid is null or s.doctor_id = $4)
            order by s.slot_date, s.start_time
            """,
            from_date, to_date, department_id, doctor_id,
        )
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


# [정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 슬롯 생성기 — doctor_schedule_rules/doctor_schedule_exceptions로부터
# 실제 appointment_slots 행을 계산한다. 관리자가 규칙·예외를 저장할 때마다 즉시 호출된다.
REGENERATION_WEEKS = 8
ACTIVE_APPOINTMENT_STATUSES = ["예약신청", "예약확정", "도착", "진료대기", "진료중"]


def _to_time(value):
    from datetime import time as _time
    if value is None or isinstance(value, _time):
        return value
    from datetime import datetime as _dt
    return _dt.strptime(value, "%H:%M").time()


def _desired_slots_for_range(rules_by_weekday: dict, exceptions_by_date: dict, today, horizon) -> set:
    """규칙·예외로부터 (slot_date, start_time) 집합을 계산한다. 규칙이 없는 요일은 슬롯을 만들지 않는다."""
    from datetime import datetime as _dt, timedelta as _td

    desired = set()
    cursor = today
    while cursor <= horizon:
        rule = rules_by_weekday.get(cursor.weekday())
        if rule is not None:
            exception = exceptions_by_date.get(cursor)
            if exception is None or not exception["is_closed"]:
                start_time = _to_time(exception["override_start_time"]) if exception and exception["override_start_time"] else _to_time(rule["start_time"])
                end_time = _to_time(exception["override_end_time"]) if exception and exception["override_end_time"] else _to_time(rule["end_time"])
                lunch_start = _to_time(rule.get("lunch_start"))
                lunch_end = _to_time(rule.get("lunch_end"))
                duration = rule["slot_duration_minutes"]
                max_daily = rule["max_daily_appointments"]

                slot_cursor = _dt.combine(cursor, start_time)
                slot_end = _dt.combine(cursor, end_time)
                step = _td(minutes=duration)
                count = 0
                while slot_cursor + step <= slot_end and count < max_daily:
                    candidate = slot_cursor.time()
                    in_lunch = lunch_start is not None and lunch_end is not None and lunch_start <= candidate < lunch_end
                    if not in_lunch:
                        desired.add((cursor, candidate))
                        count += 1
                    slot_cursor += step
        cursor += _td(days=1)
    return desired


async def _fetch_rules_and_exceptions(conn, doctor_id: UUID, today, horizon) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    rule_rows = await conn.fetch(
        "select * from doctor_schedule_rules where doctor_id = $1", doctor_id,
    )
    exception_rows = await conn.fetch(
        "select * from doctor_schedule_exceptions where doctor_id = $1 and exception_date between $2 and $3",
        doctor_id, today, horizon,
    )
    rules_by_weekday = {r["weekday"]: dict(r) for r in rule_rows}
    exceptions_by_date = {e["exception_date"]: dict(e) for e in exception_rows}
    return rules_by_weekday, exceptions_by_date


async def regenerate_slots(doctor_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext, dry_run: bool = False) -> dict:
    """새 규칙으로 앞으로 REGENERATION_WEEKS주치 슬롯을 재계산한다. 빈 슬롯만 삭제·재생성하고,
    이미 예약이 잡힌 슬롯은 건드리지 않는 대신 새 규칙에서 빠졌으면 영향받는 예약으로 보고한다."""
    if staff.role != "admin":
        raise AppError("일정 관리는 관리자만 할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)

    from datetime import date, timedelta

    today = date.today()
    horizon = today + timedelta(weeks=REGENERATION_WEEKS)

    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rules_by_weekday, exceptions_by_date = await _fetch_rules_and_exceptions(conn, doctor_id, today, horizon)
        desired = _desired_slots_for_range(rules_by_weekday, exceptions_by_date, today, horizon)

        existing_rows = await conn.fetch(
            "select id, slot_date, start_time, status from appointment_slots where doctor_id = $1 and slot_date between $2 and $3",
            doctor_id, today, horizon,
        )
        existing = {(r["slot_date"], r["start_time"]): r for r in existing_rows}

        affected_appointments = []
        for key, row in existing.items():
            if key not in desired and row["status"] == "예약됨":
                appt = await conn.fetchrow(
                    """
                    select a.id, a.reason, a.account_patient_id, a.for_patient_id,
                           p.name as patient_name, p.phone as raw_phone
                    from appointments a join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
                    where a.slot_id = $1 and a.status = any($2::text[])
                    """,
                    row["id"], ACTIVE_APPOINTMENT_STATUSES,
                )
                if appt is not None:
                    # [정합성 검토 R5-06] 원본 phone은 담지 않고 masked_phone만 포함한다.
                    appt_dict = {k: v for k, v in dict(appt).items() if k != "raw_phone"}
                    affected_appointments.append({
                        **appt_dict, "masked_phone": mask_phone(appt["raw_phone"]),
                        "slot_date": key[0], "start_time": key[1],
                    })

        slots_to_remove = [row["id"] for key, row in existing.items() if key not in desired and row["status"] == "빈시간"]
        slots_to_add = [key for key in desired if key not in existing]

        if not dry_run:
            for slot_id in slots_to_remove:
                await conn.execute("delete from appointment_slots where id = $1", slot_id)
            for slot_date, start_time in slots_to_add:
                await conn.execute(
                    "insert into appointment_slots (doctor_id, slot_date, start_time, status) values ($1, $2, $3, '빈시간')",
                    doctor_id, slot_date, start_time,
                )
            if affected_appointments:
                # 예약은 그대로 두고 플래그만 세운다 — 개별 재조정·취소는 접수직원이 별도로 처리한다.
                await conn.execute(
                    "update appointments set needs_rescheduling = true where id = any($1::uuid[])",
                    [a["id"] for a in affected_appointments],
                )

    # [정합성 검토 R2-05] needs_rescheduling=true로 전환되는 시점에 일정변경 알림이 호출되지 않던
    # 문제를 고친다. dry_run(저장 전 미리보기)에서는 아직 아무것도 바뀌지 않았으므로 보내지 않는다.
    # notify_patient는 (appointment_id, "changed")로 중복을 막으므로, 관리자가 실수로 같은 규칙을
    # 여러 번 저장해도 이미 알림을 받은 예약에는 다시 보내지 않는다.
    if not dry_run:
        for appt in affected_appointments:
            target_name = appt["patient_name"] if appt["for_patient_id"] != appt["account_patient_id"] else None
            try:
                await notification_service.notify_patient(
                    appt["account_patient_id"], "changed", target_name=target_name, appointment_id=appt["id"],
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

    return {
        "affected_appointments": affected_appointments,
        "slots_added": len(slots_to_add),
        "slots_removed": len(slots_to_remove),
    }


async def preview_rule_change(doctor_id: UUID, weekday: int, rule: dict, staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    """저장하지 않고, 후보 규칙(rule)을 적용했을 때 영향받는 예약만 계산한다(SchedulePage 저장 전 경고용)."""
    if staff.role != "admin":
        raise AppError("일정 관리는 관리자만 할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)

    from datetime import date, timedelta

    today = date.today()
    horizon = today + timedelta(weeks=REGENERATION_WEEKS)

    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rules_by_weekday, exceptions_by_date = await _fetch_rules_and_exceptions(conn, doctor_id, today, horizon)
        rules_by_weekday[weekday] = rule
        desired = _desired_slots_for_range(rules_by_weekday, exceptions_by_date, today, horizon)

        existing_rows = await conn.fetch(
            "select id, slot_date, start_time, status from appointment_slots where doctor_id = $1 and slot_date between $2 and $3",
            doctor_id, today, horizon,
        )

        affected_appointments = []
        for row in existing_rows:
            key = (row["slot_date"], row["start_time"])
            if key not in desired and row["status"] == "예약됨":
                appt = await conn.fetchrow(
                    """
                    select a.id, a.reason, p.name as patient_name, p.phone as raw_phone
                    from appointments a join patients p on p.id = a.for_patient_id
                    where a.slot_id = $1 and a.status = any($2::text[])
                    """,
                    row["id"], ACTIVE_APPOINTMENT_STATUSES,
                )
                if appt is not None:
                    # [정합성 검토 R5-06] 원본 phone은 담지 않고 masked_phone만 포함한다.
                    appt_dict = {k: v for k, v in dict(appt).items() if k != "raw_phone"}
                    affected_appointments.append({
                        **appt_dict, "masked_phone": mask_phone(appt["raw_phone"]),
                        "slot_date": key[0], "start_time": key[1],
                    })
        return affected_appointments
```

- [ ] **Step 4: "처리 필요" 대시보드 카드 연동**

`backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py`의 `get_today_summary`(R2-07에서 이미 수정한 함수)에서, 지금까지 항상 `0`을 반환하던 자리를 실제 개수로 교체한다:

```python
        needs_rescheduling_count = await conn.fetchval(
            """
            select count(*) from appointments
            where needs_rescheduling
              and status in ('예약신청', '예약확정', '도착', '진료대기', '진료중')
            """
        )
```

그리고 반환 dict의 `"affected_appointments_count": 0`을 `"affected_appointments_count": needs_rescheduling_count`로 바꾼다. (`regenerate_slots`가 영향받는 예약에 `needs_rescheduling = true`를 세우는 부분은 Step 3에서 이미 구현했다.)

`backend/app/services/appointment_service.py`의 `reschedule_appointment`(Task 2)이 실행되면 그 예약은 더 이상 처리 대기 상태가 아니므로, 슬롯 갱신 UPDATE 문에 `needs_rescheduling = false`를 추가한다:

```python
        await conn.execute(
            "update appointments set slot_id = $1, doctor_id = $2, updated_at = now(), needs_rescheduling = false where id = $3",
            new_slot_id, new_slot["doctor_id"], appointment_id,
        )
```

`backend/tests/test_dashboard_service.py`에 추가:
```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_today_summary_reports_needs_rescheduling_count(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 슬롯 재계산으로 플래그된 예약이 '처리 필요' 카드 수치에 반영된다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    receptionist = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="receptionist")
    doctor = await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('내과') returning id")
    await db_conn.execute("update staff set department_id = $1 where id = $2", dept_id, doctor["staff_id"])
    patient_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )
    from app.services import schedule_service
    from datetime import date, timedelta
    target_date = date.today() + timedelta(days=5)
    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "18:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": None, "lunch_end": None, "max_daily_appointments": 20, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    slot_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "select id from appointment_slots where doctor_id = $1 and slot_date = $2 and start_time = '17:30'",
        doctor["staff_id"], target_date,
    )
    await db_conn.execute("update appointment_slots set status = '예약됨' where id = $1", slot_id)
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into appointments (slot_id, account_patient_id, for_patient_id, department_id, doctor_id, status, source, created_by)
        values ($1, $2, $2, $3, $4, '예약확정', 'staff', $5)
        """,
        slot_id, patient_id, dept_id, doctor["staff_id"], receptionist["staff_id"],
    )

    await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(
        doctor["staff_id"], weekday=target_date.weekday(),
        rule={"start_time": "09:00", "end_time": "18:00", "slot_duration_minutes": 30,
              "lunch_start": None, "lunch_end": None, "max_daily_appointments": 5, "booking_deadline": None},
        staff=admin,
    )
    await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor["staff_id"], admin)

    summary = await dashboard_service.get_today_summary(admin)
    assert summary["affected_appointments_count"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 5: schedule 라우터 작성**

`backend/app/routers/schedule.py`:
```python
from datetime import date
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import schedule_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["schedule"])


class CreateDepartmentRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str


@router.get("/departments")
async def get_departments() -> list[dict]:
    return await schedule_service.list_departments()


@router.post("/departments")
async def post_department(
    body: CreateDepartmentRequest, staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> dict:
    dept_id = await schedule_service.create_department(body.name, staff)
    return {"id": str(dept_id)}


class ScheduleRuleRequest(BaseModel):
    start_time: str
    end_time: str
    slot_duration_minutes: int
    lunch_start: str | None = None
    lunch_end: str | None = None
    max_daily_appointments: int
    booking_deadline: str | None = None


@router.get("/doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-rules")
async def get_schedule_rules(doctor_id: UUID) -> list[dict]:
    return await schedule_service.list_schedule_rules(doctor_id)


@router.put("/doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-rules/{weekday}")
async def put_schedule_rule(
    doctor_id: UUID, weekday: int, body: ScheduleRuleRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> dict:
    rule_id = await schedule_service.upsert_schedule_rule(doctor_id, weekday, body.model_dump(), staff)
    # [정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 규칙 저장 즉시 앞으로 8주치 슬롯을 재계산한다.
    # 관리자의 저장 자체는 여기서 끝나고, 영향받는 예약의 개별 재조정은 접수직원이 별도로 처리한다.
    regeneration = await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor_id, staff)
    return {"id": str(rule_id), "affected_appointments": regeneration["affected_appointments"]}


@router.post("/doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-rules/{weekday}/preview")
async def preview_schedule_rule(
    doctor_id: UUID, weekday: int, body: ScheduleRuleRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> dict:
    """저장 전 미리보기 — SchedulePage가 "저장" 버튼을 누르기 전, 영향받는 예약 목록으로 경고를 보여줄 때 쓴다."""
    affected = await schedule_service.preview_rule_change(doctor_id, weekday, body.model_dump(), staff)
    return {"affected_appointments": affected}


@router.get("/doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-exceptions")
async def get_schedule_exceptions(doctor_id: UUID) -> list[dict]:
    return await schedule_service.list_schedule_exceptions(doctor_id)


class ScheduleExceptionRequest(BaseModel):
    exception_date: date
    is_closed: bool
    override_start_time: str | None = None
    override_end_time: str | None = None
    notify_appointment_ids: list[UUID] = []


@router.post("/doctors/{doctor_id}/schedule-exceptions")
async def post_schedule_exception(
    doctor_id: UUID, body: ScheduleExceptionRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> dict:
    exception_id = await schedule_service.create_schedule_exception(
        doctor_id, str(body.exception_date), body.is_closed,
        body.override_start_time, body.override_end_time, body.notify_appointment_ids, staff,
    )
    # [정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 예외 저장 후에도 슬롯을 재계산한다 — notify_appointment_ids로
    # 선택하지 않은 나머지 영향받는 예약은 여기서 다시 보고되어 "처리 필요" 목록에 남는다.
    regeneration = await schedule_service.regenerate_slots(doctor_id, staff)
    return {"id": str(exception_id), "affected_appointments": regeneration["affected_appointments"]}


@router.get("/calendar-slots")
async def get_calendar_slots(
    from_: date, to: date,
    department_id: UUID | None = None, doctor_id: UUID | None = None,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "doctor", "admin")),
) -> list[dict]:
    return await schedule_service.list_calendar_slots(department_id, doctor_id, str(from_), str(to), staff)
```

`backend/app/main.py`에 라우터 등록:
```python
from app.routers import schedule

app.include_router(schedule.router)
```

```tsx
}

describe("SchedulePage", () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 2: schedule API 클라이언트 + SchedulePage 실구현**

`frontend/src/api/schedule.ts`:
```typescript
import { apiFetch } from "./httpClient";

export type Department = { id: string; name: string };

export function listDepartments() {
  return apiFetch<Department[]>("/departments");
}

export function createDepartment(name: string) {
  return apiFetch<{ id: string }>("/departments", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ name }) });
}

export function listScheduleExceptions(doctorId: string) {
  return apiFetch<Array<{ id: string; exception_date: string; is_closed: boolean }>>(
    `/doctors/${doctorId}/schedule-exceptions`,
  );
}

export function createScheduleException(
  doctorId: string,
  body: { exception_date: string; is_closed: boolean; notify_appointment_ids: string[] },
) {
  return apiFetch<{ id: string }>(`/doctors/${doctorId}/schedule-exceptions`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

// [정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 슬롯 생성기와 연동되는 스케줄 규칙 저장/미리보기.
export type ScheduleRuleInput = {
  start_time: string;
  end_time: string;
  slot_duration_minutes: number;
  lunch_start?: string;
  lunch_end?: string;
  max_daily_appointments: number;
  booking_deadline?: string;
};

export type AffectedAppointment = {
  id: string;
  patient_name: string;
  masked_phone: string; // [정합성 검토 R5-06]
  slot_date: string;
  start_time: string;
};

export function previewScheduleRule(doctorId: string, weekday: number, rule: ScheduleRuleInput) {
  return apiFetch<{ affected_appointments: AffectedAppointment[] }>(
    `/doctors/${doctorId}/schedule-rules/${weekday}/preview`,
    { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(rule) },
  );
}

export function saveScheduleRule(doctorId: string, weekday: number, rule: ScheduleRuleInput) {
  return apiFetch<{ id: string; affected_appointments: AffectedAppointment[] }>(
    `/doctors/${doctorId}/schedule-rules/${weekday}`,
    { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify(rule) },
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/features/admin/SchedulePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import {
  createDepartment, createScheduleException, listDepartments,
  previewScheduleRule, saveScheduleRule, type AffectedAppointment, type ScheduleRuleInput,
} from "../../api/schedule";
import { AffectedAppointmentsDialog } from "./AffectedAppointmentsDialog";

export function SchedulePage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: departments } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["departments"], queryFn: listDepartments });
  const [newDeptName, setNewDeptName] = useState("");
  const [created, setCreated] = useState(false);

  const [selectedDoctorId, setSelectedDoctorId] = useState("doc1");
  const [closedDate, setClosedDate] = useState("");
  const [checkingDoctorId, setCheckingDoctorId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // [정합성 검토 R3-01/R3-02] 요일별 스케줄 규칙 — 지금은 월요일(weekday=1) 고정 입력으로 단순화한다.
  // 요일 선택 UI 확장은 이번 갭 범위 밖(추후 개선).
  const RULE_WEEKDAY = 1;
  const [ruleForm, setRuleForm] = useState<ScheduleRuleInput>({
    start_time: "", end_time: "", slot_duration_minutes: 20, max_daily_appointments: 0,
  });
  const [ruleWarning, setRuleWarning] = useState<AffectedAppointment[] | null>(null);
  const [ruleSaved, setRuleSaved] = useState(false);

  async function handleCreateDepartment() {
    await createDepartment(newDeptName);
    setNewDeptName("");
    setCreated(true);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["departments"] });
  }

  async function handlePreviewRule() {
    const result = await previewScheduleRule(selectedDoctorId, RULE_WEEKDAY, ruleForm);
    setRuleSaved(false);
    if (result.affected_appointments.length === 0) {
      await handleSaveRule();
      return;
    }
    setRuleWarning(result.affected_appointments);
  }

  async function handleSaveRule() {
    await saveScheduleRule(selectedDoctorId, RULE_WEEKDAY, ruleForm);
    setRuleWarning(null);
    setRuleSaved(true);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <section aria-label="진료과 관리">
        {departments?.map((d) => <p key={d.id}>{d.name}</p>)}
        <label>
          새 진료과 이름
          <input value={newDeptName} onChange={(e) => setNewDeptName(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <button type="button" onClick={handleCreateDepartment}>
          진료과 추가
        </button>
        {created && <p>추가되었습니다.</p>}
      </section>

      <section aria-label="의사별 스케줄">
        <label>
          시작 시간
          <input value={ruleForm.start_time} onChange={(e) => setRuleForm({ ...ruleForm, start_time: e.target.value })} />
        </label>
        <label>
          종료 시간
          <input value={ruleForm.end_time} onChange={(e) => setRuleForm({ ...ruleForm, end_time: e.target.value })} />
        </label>
        <label>
          하루 최대 인원
          <input
            type="number"
            value={ruleForm.max_daily_appointments || ""}
            onChange={(e) => setRuleForm({ ...ruleForm, max_daily_appointments: Number(e.target.value) })}
          />
        </label>
        <button type="button" onClick={handlePreviewRule}>
          스케줄 저장
        </button>
        {ruleSaved && <p>일정이 저장되었습니다.</p>}

        {ruleWarning && (
          <div role="dialog">
            <p>영향받는 예약 {ruleWarning.length}건</p>
            <p>
              새 규칙에서는 사라지는 시간대에 이미 예약이 있습니다. 저장해도 해당 예약은 취소되지 않고
              "처리 필요" 목록에 남아 접수직원이 개별적으로 재예약·취소를 진행합니다.
            </p>
            {ruleWarning.map((a) => (
              <p key={a.id}>
                {a.patient_name} ({a.masked_phone}) — {a.slot_date} {a.start_time}
              </p>
            ))}
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setRuleWarning(null)}>
              취소
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleSaveRule}>
              그래도 저장
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section aria-label="특정일 예외">
        <label>
          휴진일
          <input type="date" value={closedDate} onChange={(e) => setClosedDate(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setCheckingDoctorId(selectedDoctorId)}>
          휴진 저장
        </button>
      </section>

      {checkingDoctorId && (
        <AffectedAppointmentsDialog
          doctorId={checkingDoctorId}
          // [정합성 검토 R3-02] 아직 저장하지 않은 휴진일(closedDate)을 후보값으로 넘겨
          // 저장 버튼을 누르기 전에 실제로 영향받을 예약을 정확히 미리 보여준다.
          candidateException={{ exceptionDate: closedDate, isClosed: true }}
          onCancel={() => setCheckingDoctorId(null)}
          onConfirm={async (selectedIds) => {
            await createScheduleException(checkingDoctorId, {
              exception_date: closedDate,
              is_closed: true,
              notify_appointment_ids: selectedIds,
            });
            setCheckingDoctorId(null);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Expected: PASS

- [ ] **Step 4: 의사 진료화면에 응급표시 반영 (스펙이 요구한 3곳 중 마지막 1곳)**

`frontend/src/features/doctorConsole/DoctorConsolePage.tsx` 수정 — `isUrgent` prop 추가(`patientId`는 Task 11 [2026-07-28 재검증]에서 이미 필수 prop으로 추가돼 있다):
```tsx
export function DoctorConsolePage({
  doctorId, appointmentId, patientId, isUrgent,
}: { doctorId: string; appointmentId: string; patientId: string; isUrgent?: boolean }) {
  // ...기존 상태 선언과 4개 패널(DoctorQueuePanel 등, Task 11 Step 4.5)은 그대로 유지...
  return (
    <div>
      {isUrgent && <p>응급/주의</p>}
```

`isUrgent`는 `dashboard_service.get_queue_today`(1단계 Task 13)가 이미 반환하는 `is_urgent_flag`를 라우트 진입 시 함께 넘겨받아 전달한다(아래 App.tsx의 `DoctorConsoleRoute` 참고). 이로써 대기목록·오늘현황·의사화면 3곳 모두에 응급표시가 반영된다(스펙 요구 충족).

- [ ] **Step 5: App.tsx에 전체 라우트·사이드바 연결**

> **[정합성 검토 R3-04] 순서 주의:** 아래 코드는 `StaffAdminPage`를 import한다 — 이 컴포넌트는 이 Task 뒤에 나오는 **Task 19**에서 만든다. Task 19를 먼저 끝내고 이 Step을 적용하거나, 이 Step에서는 import를 주석 처리해뒀다가 Task 19 완료 후 되살린다. (Task 17→18 SchedulePage 참조와 같은 순서로, 이번엔 18→19 관계다.)

`frontend/src/App.tsx`:
```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"; // [2026-07-28 재검증] useQuery 추가
import { BrowserRouter, Link, Navigate, Route, Routes, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import { apiFetch } from "./api/httpClient"; // [2026-07-28 재검증]
import { AccessLogPage } from "./features/admin/AccessLogPage";
import { SchedulePage } from "./features/admin/SchedulePage";
import { StaffAdminPage } from "./features/admin/StaffAdminPage";
import { StatsPage } from "./features/admin/StatsPage";
import { CalendarPage } from "./features/calendar/CalendarPage";
import { CancellationRequestsPage } from "./features/cancellationRequests/CancellationRequestsPage";
import { DoctorConsolePage } from "./features/doctorConsole/DoctorConsolePage";
import { PatientDetailPage } from "./features/patients/PatientDetailPage";
import { QueuePage } from "./features/queue/QueuePage";
import { TodayPage } from "./features/today/TodayPage";
import { useAuth } from "./auth/useAuth";
import { AuthProvider } from "./auth/AuthProvider";
import { RequireRole } from "./auth/RequireRole";
import { queryClient } from "./lib/queryClient";

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL as string;

function DoctorConsoleRoute() {
  const { appointmentId } = useParams();
  const { staff } = useAuth();
  // [2026-07-28 재검증] Task 11이 DoctorConsolePage에 필수로 요구하는 patientId는
  // /queue-today(Task 13, get_queue_today가 이제 patient_id를 함께 반환)에서 현재 appointmentId에
  // 해당하는 행을 찾아 얻는다 — DoctorQueuePanel(Task 11)과 동일한 queryKey("queue-today")를 써서
  // react-query 캐시를 공유하므로 중복 요청이 발생하지 않는다.
  const { data: queue } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["queue-today"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<{ id: string; patient_id: string; is_urgent_flag: boolean }[]>("/queue-today"),
  });
  const currentRow = queue?.find((row) => row.id === appointmentId);
  if (!staff || !appointmentId) return null;
  if (!currentRow) return <p>대기 목록에서 이 예약을 찾을 수 없습니다.</p>;
  return (
    <DoctorConsolePage
      doctorId={staff.staffId}
      appointmentId={appointmentId}
      patientId={currentRow.patient_id}
      isUrgent={currentRow.is_urgent_flag}
    />
  );
}

function Sidebar() {
  const { staff } = useAuth();
  if (!staff) return null;
  return (
    <nav>
      <Link to="/today">오늘의 현황</Link>
      <Link to="/queue">대기 목록</Link>
      <Link to="/calendar">예약 캘린더</Link>
      {staff.role === "doctor" && <Link to="/doctor/console">진료 화면</Link>}
      {staff.role === "admin" && (
        <>
          <Link to="/admin/staff">직원 관리</Link>
          <Link to="/admin/schedule">일정관리</Link>
          <Link to="/admin/stats">운영 통계</Link>
          <Link to="/admin/access-logs">열람 기록</Link>
        </>
      )}
      <Link to="/cancellation-requests">취소요청 대기열</Link>
    </nav>
  );
}

export function App() {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <AuthProvider apiBaseUrl={API_BASE_URL}>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <Sidebar />
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/today" replace />} />
            <Route
              path="/today"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["receptionist", "doctor", "admin"]}><TodayPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/queue"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["receptionist", "doctor", "admin"]}><QueuePage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/calendar"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["receptionist", "admin"]}><CalendarPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/patients/:id"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["receptionist", "doctor", "admin"]}><PatientDetailPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/doctor/console/:appointmentId"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["doctor"]}><DoctorConsoleRoute /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/staff"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["admin"]}><StaffAdminPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/schedule"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["admin"]}><SchedulePage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/stats"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["admin"]}><StatsPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/access-logs"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["admin"]}><AccessLogPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
            <Route
              path="/cancellation-requests"
              element={<RequireRole roles={["receptionist", "admin"]}><CancellationRequestsPage /></RequireRole>}
            />
          </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </AuthProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
```

> 대기 목록(Task 9)에서 진료 시작 버튼을 누르면 `/doctor/console/:appointmentId`로 이동하도록 링크를 추가한다 — 이 부분은 QueuePage의 기존 버튼 핸들러에 `navigate(...)` 한 줄을 더하는 수준이라 별도 스텝 없이 이 커밋에 포함한다.

## Task 19: 관리자 — 직원 계정 관리 화면 (`/admin/staff`)

> 정합성 검토에서 확인된 갭(R3-04): 스펙(`staff-web-design.md` 섹션 7)은 `/admin/staff`에서 직원·의사 계정을 관리하도록 하지만, 구현 계획에는 1단계 백엔드 API(`POST /staff`, `PATCH /staff/{id}/deactivate`, `GET /staff` — 1단계 Task 12·17에서 R3-04로 추가)만 있고 화면·라우트·API 클라이언트가 없었다. 이 Task가 화면을 만들고, Task 18의 `App.tsx`가 이미 라우트(`/admin/staff`)와 사이드바 링크를 걸어뒀다.

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/api/staff.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/admin/StaffAdminPage.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/src/features/admin/StaffAdminPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /staff`, `POST /staff`, `PATCH /staff/{staff_id}/deactivate`(1단계 Task 12·17), `useAuth`(Task 4)
- Produces: `<StaffAdminPage>`(직원 목록, 초대 폼, 중지 확인 다이얼로그) — Task 18의 `App.tsx`가 `/admin/staff` 라우트로 이 컴포넌트를 가져다 쓴다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`frontend/src/features/admin/StaffAdminPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { HttpResponse, http } from "msw";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { server } from "../../test/msw/server";
import { StaffAdminPage } from "./StaffAdminPage";

describe("StaffAdminPage", () => {
  it("lists staff and shows inactive members as 중지됨", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/staff", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([
          { id: "s1", name: "김관리", role: "admin", department_id: null, is_active: true },
          { id: "s2", name: "박접수", role: "receptionist", department_id: null, is_active: false },
        ]),
      ),
    );

    render(<StaffAdminPage />);

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("김관리")).toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(screen.getByText("박접수")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("중지됨")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("invites a new doctor with a department", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/staff", () => HttpResponse.json([])),
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/departments", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([{ id: "d1", name: "내과" }]),
      ),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/staff", () => HttpResponse.json({ staff_id: "s3" })),
    );

    render(<StaffAdminPage />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "초대" })).toBeInTheDocument());

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("이메일"), "new-doctor@test.local");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("이름"), "김의사");
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("역할"), "doctor");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText("소속 진료과")).toBeInTheDocument());
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("소속 진료과"), "d1");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "초대" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("초대했습니다.")).toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it("blocks doctor invite without a department", async () => {
    // [정합성 검토 R3-04] 의사 초대 시 진료과 미선택을 서버 요청 전에 걸러낸다.
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/staff", () => HttpResponse.json([])),

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("이메일"), "new-doctor@test.local");
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("이름"), "김의사");
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("역할"), "doctor");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "초대" }));

    expect(await screen.findByText("의사는 소속 진료과를 선택해야 합니다.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("resends an invite for an active staff member", async () => {
    // [정합성 검토 R3-04] 재초대 버튼이 별도 엔드포인트를 호출해야 한다.
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/staff", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([{ id: "s2", name: "박접수", role: "receptionist", department_id: null, is_active: true }]),
      ),
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/departments", () => HttpResponse.json([])),
      http.post("http://localhost:8000/staff/s2/resend-invite", () => HttpResponse.json({ status: "resent" })),
    );

    render(<StaffAdminPage />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("박접수")).toBeInTheDocument());

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "재초대" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("재초대 이메일을 보냈습니다.")).toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it("shows a confirmation dialog before deactivating, and hides the button for self", async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/me", () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ staff_id: "s1", role: "admin", department_id: null }),
      ),
      http.get("http://localhost:8000/staff", () =>
        HttpResponse.json([
          { id: "s1", name: "김관리(나)", role: "admin", department_id: null, is_active: true },
          { id: "s2", name: "박접수", role: "receptionist", department_id: null, is_active: true },
        ]),
      ),
      http.patch("http://localhost:8000/staff/s2/deactivate", () => HttpResponse.json({ status: "deactivated" })),
    );

    render(<StaffAdminPage />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("박접수")).toBeInTheDocument());

    // 본인(s1) 행에는 중지 버튼이 없다 — 백엔드가 어차피 거부하지만 화면에서도 애초에 숨긴다.
    const selfRow = screen.getByText("김관리(나)").closest("li")!;
    expect(within(selfRow).queryByRole("button", { name: "중지" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    const otherRow = screen.getByText("박접수").closest("li")!;
    await userEvent.click(within(otherRow).getByRole("button", { name: "중지" }));
    expect(screen.getByText("박접수 계정을 중지할까요?")).toBeInTheDocument();

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "중지 확정" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText("박접수 계정을 중지할까요?")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: staff API 클라이언트 작성**

`frontend/src/api/staff.ts`:
```typescript
import { apiFetch } from "./httpClient";

export type Staff = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  role: "receptionist" | "doctor" | "admin";
  department_id: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
};

export function listStaff() {
  return apiFetch<Staff[]>("/staff");
}

export function inviteStaff(body: { email: string; name: string; role: string; department_id?: string | null }) {
  return apiFetch<{ staff_id: string }>("/staff", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(body) });
}

export function deactivateStaff(staffId: string) {
  return apiFetch<{ status: string }>(`/staff/${staffId}/deactivate`, { method: "PATCH" });
}

export function resendInvite(staffId: string) {
  // [정합성 검토 R3-04] 초대 이메일 재발송
  return apiFetch<{ status: string }>(`/staff/${staffId}/resend-invite`, { method: "POST" });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: StaffAdminPage 구현**

`frontend/src/features/admin/StaffAdminPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { deactivateStaff, inviteStaff, listStaff, resendInvite } from "../../api/staff";
import { listDepartments } from "../../api/schedule";
import { useAuth } from "../../auth/useAuth";

export function StaffAdminPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { staff: me } = useAuth();
  const { data: staffList } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["staff"], queryFn: listStaff });
  // [정합성 검토 R3-04] 의사 초대 시 소속 진료과를 고르는 드롭다운 — 다른 관리자 화면(SchedulePage)이
  // 이미 쓰는 목록 API를 재사용한다.
  const { data: departments } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["departments"], queryFn: listDepartments });

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("receptionist");
  const [departmentId, setDepartmentId] = useState("");
  const [invited, setInvited] = useState(false);
  const [inviteError, setInviteError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [confirmTarget, setConfirmTarget] = useState<{ id: string; name: string } | null>(null);
  const [resentId, setResentId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleInvite() {
    setInviteError(null);
    if (role === "doctor" && !departmentId) {
      setInviteError("의사는 소속 진료과를 선택해야 합니다.");
      return;
    }
    try {
      await inviteStaff({ email, name, role, department_id: role === "doctor" ? departmentId : null });
      setEmail("");
      setName("");
      setDepartmentId("");
      setInvited(true);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staff"] });
    } catch {
      // 부분 실패(초대 이메일 발송 실패 등)도 여기로 들어온다 — 재시도는 같은 폼 재제출로 처리한다.
      setInviteError("초대에 실패했습니다. 다시 시도해주세요.");
    }
  }

  async function handleConfirmDeactivate() {
    if (!confirmTarget) return;
    await deactivateStaff(confirmTarget.id);
    setConfirmTarget(null);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staff"] });
  }

  async function handleResendInvite(staffId: string) {
    setResentId(null);
    try {
      await resendInvite(staffId);
      setResentId(staffId);
    } catch {
      setInviteError("재초대에 실패했습니다. 이미 초대를 수락한 계정일 수 있습니다.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <section aria-label="직원 초대">
        <label>
          이메일
          <input value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <label>
          이름
          <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <label>
          역할
          <select value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="receptionist">접수직원</option>
            <option value="doctor">의사</option>
            <option value="admin">관리자</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        {role === "doctor" && (
          <label>
            소속 진료과
            <select value={departmentId} onChange={(e) => setDepartmentId(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">진료과를 선택하세요</option>
              {departments?.map((d: { id: string; name: string }) => (
                <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.name}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        )}
        <button type="button" onClick={handleInvite}>
          초대
        </button>
        {invited && <p>초대했습니다.</p>}
        {inviteError && <p role="alert">{inviteError}</p>}
      </section>

      <ul aria-label="직원 목록">
        {staffList?.map((s) => (
          <li key={s.id}>
            {s.name}
            {s.id === me?.staffId && "(나)"}
            {!s.is_active && " 중지됨"}
            {s.id !== me?.staffId && s.is_active && (
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmTarget({ id: s.id, name: s.name })}>
                중지
              </button>
            )}
            {/* [정합성 검토 R3-04] 초대 이메일을 못 받았거나 만료된 경우를 위한 재초대 버튼.
                수락 여부를 별도로 추적하지 않으므로 활성 직원 누구에게나 노출하고, 이미 수락한
                계정에 호출하면 서버가 409로 안내한다. */}
            {s.is_active && (
              <button type="button" onClick={() => handleResendInvite(s.id)}>
                재초대
              </button>
            )}
            {resentId === s.id && <span> 재초대 이메일을 보냈습니다.</span>}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      {confirmTarget && (
        <div role="dialog">
          <p>{confirmTarget.name} 계정을 중지할까요?</p>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmTarget(null)}>
            취소
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={handleConfirmDeactivate}>
            중지 확정
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## Task 20: [정합성 검토 R4-04] 예약번호(booking_code) 검색 + QR 스캔 접수 화면

3단계(환자 앱) 홈 화면이 이제 UUID 대신 6자리 `booking_code`를 QR/텍스트로 보여준다(3단계 Task 22). 직원 웹은 지금 전화번호+생년월일 검색만 지원하므로, 이 코드로 접수를 열 수 있는 경로가 없다 — QR 스캔과 번호 직접 입력 두 경로가 검색 함수 하나를 공유하도록 추가한다.

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/services/appointment_service.py` (`find_by_booking_code` 추가)
- Modify: `backend/app/routers/appointments.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_appointment_service.py` (추가)
- Create: `frontend/src/api/bookingCode.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/checkin/BookingCodeCheckInPage.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/App.tsx` (`/checkin` 라우트 + 사이드바 링크)
- Test: `frontend/src/features/checkin/BookingCodeCheckInPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.db.pool.acquire_as`(1단계 Task 9), `appointments.booking_code/booking_code_expires_at`(1단계 Task 5)
- Produces: `app.services.appointment_service.find_by_booking_code(code: str, staff: StaffContext) -> UUID | None`(만료·미존재는 구분 없이 `None`), `GET /appointments/find-by-code?code=`, `<BookingCodeCheckInPage />` — `/checkin` 라우트

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성 — find_by_booking_code**

`backend/tests/test_appointment_service.py`에 추가:
```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_find_by_booking_code_returns_valid_appointment(db_conn):
    ctx = await _seed_base(db_conn)
    appointment_id = await appointment_service.create_appointment(
        staff=ctx["receptionist"], account_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"], for_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"],
        department_id=ctx["dept_id"], doctor_id=ctx["doctor"].id, reason="감기",
        source="app", initial_status="예약확정",
    )
    code = await db_conn.fetchval("select booking_code from appointments where id = $1", appointment_id)

    found_id = await appointment_service.find_by_booking_code(code, ctx["receptionist"])
    assert found_id == appointment_id


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_find_by_booking_code_returns_none_when_expired(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R4-04] 만료된 코드는 실제 사유(취소/부도 등)를 노출하지 않고 그냥 못 찾은 것으로 처리한다."""
    ctx = await _seed_base(db_conn)
    appointment_id = await appointment_service.create_appointment(
        staff=ctx["receptionist"], account_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"], for_patient_id=ctx["patient_id"],
        department_id=ctx["dept_id"], doctor_id=ctx["doctor"].id, reason="감기",
        source="app", initial_status="예약확정",
    )
    code = await db_conn.fetchval("select booking_code from appointments where id = $1", appointment_id)
    await db_conn.execute(
        "update appointments set status = '환자취소' where id = $1", appointment_id,
    )  # 종료 상태 트리거가 booking_code를 이미 비웠을 것이다

    found_id = await appointment_service.find_by_booking_code(code, ctx["receptionist"])
    assert found_id is None
```

- [ ] **Step 2: find_by_booking_code 구현**

`backend/app/services/appointment_service.py` 파일 끝에 추가:
```python
async def find_by_booking_code(code: str, staff: StaffContext) -> UUID | None:
    """[정합성 검토 R4-04] 유효(미만료)한 코드만 반환한다. 만료된 코드는 실제 사유(취소/부도/경과)를
    노출하지 않기 위해 단순히 None(못 찾음)으로 처리한다 — 호출부(라우터)가 동일한 안내 문구를 쓴다."""
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        return await conn.fetchval(
            "select id from appointments where booking_code = $1 and booking_code_expires_at > now()",
            code.strip().upper(),
        )
```

```python
@router.get("/find-by-code", response_model=FindByCodeResponse)
async def find_by_code(
    code: str,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> FindByCodeResponse:
    appointment_id = await appointment_service.find_by_booking_code(code, staff)
```

```typescript
import { apiClient } from "./client";

  const res = await apiClient.get<{ appointment_id: string | null }>("/appointments/find-by-code", {
    params: { code },
  });
  return res.data.appointment_id;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: QrCodeScanner 컴포넌트 구현 (카메라 스캐너)**

`npm install html5-qrcode` (프로덕션 의존성 추가 — `frontend/package.json`).

`frontend/src/features/checkin/QrCodeScanner.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

type Props = {
  onDecoded: (text: string) => void;
  onError?: (message: string) => void;
};

// [정합성 검토 R4-04] html5-qrcode로 카메라 프레임을 디코드해 QR에 담긴 예약번호를 읽는다.
// 이전 버전은 이 컴포넌트 자체가 없어 "카메라 접근 라이브러리로 처리한다"는 주석만 있었다.
export function QrCodeScanner({ onDecoded, onError }: Props) {
  const containerId = useRef(`qr-scanner-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`);
  const scannedRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let scanner: import("html5-qrcode").Html5Qrcode | null = null;
    let cancelled = false;

    import("html5-qrcode").then(({ Html5Qrcode }) => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      scanner = new Html5Qrcode(containerId.current);
      scanner
        .start(
          { facingMode: "environment" },
          { fps: 10, qrbox: 250 },
          (decodedText) => {
            // 같은 프레임 스트림에서 여러 번 콜백이 올 수 있으므로 첫 인식 이후는 무시한다.
            if (scannedRef.current) return;
            scannedRef.current = true;
            onDecoded(decodedText);
          },
          () => {
            // 프레임마다 못 읽으면 호출되는 정상적인 콜백 — 매번 오류로 취급하지 않는다.
          },
        )
        .catch(() => {
          onError?.("카메라를 시작할 수 없습니다. 카메라 권한을 확인해주세요.");
        });
    });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      scanner?.stop().catch(() => {});
    };
  }, [onDecoded, onError]);

  return <div id={containerId.current} aria-label="QR 스캐너" style={{ width: 300 }} />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: BookingCodeCheckInPage 구현 (직접 입력 + QR 스캔 공유)**

`frontend/src/features/checkin/BookingCodeCheckInPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { findByBookingCode } from "../../api/bookingCode";
import { QrCodeScanner } from "./QrCodeScanner";

export function BookingCodeCheckInPage() {
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false);
  const [found, setFound] = useState(false);
  const [scannerError, setScannerError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // [정합성 검토 R4-04] QR 스캔과 직접 입력이 같은 검색 함수를 공유한다.
  const handleSearch = async (value: string) => {
    setNotFound(false);
    setFound(false);
    const appointmentId = await findByBookingCode(value);
    if (appointmentId) {
      setFound(true);
      navigate(`/patients/appointments/${appointmentId}`);
    } else {
      setNotFound(true);
    }
  };

  const handleDecoded = (text: string) => {
    setScanning(false);
    const decodedCode = text.trim().toUpperCase();
    setCode(decodedCode);
    handleSearch(decodedCode);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <h1>예약번호로 접수</h1>
      <label htmlFor="booking-code">예약번호</label>
      <input id="booking-code" value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())} />
      <button onClick={() => handleSearch(code)}>조회</button>

      <button type="button" onClick={() => setScanning((v) => !v)}>
        {scanning ? "QR 스캔 중지" : "QR 스캔 시작"}
      </button>
      {scanning && (
        <QrCodeScanner onDecoded={handleDecoded} onError={(message) => setScannerError(message)} />
      )}
      {scannerError && <p role="alert">{scannerError}</p>}

      {found && <p>예약을 찾았습니다.</p>}
      {notFound && <p>만료되었거나 존재하지 않는 예약번호입니다.</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Expected: 2개 테스트 모두 PASS(QR 스캐너는 카메라 API에 의존하므로 vitest에서는 `QrCodeScanner`를 직접 렌더링하지 않는 기존 두 테스트만 실행 대상 — 카메라 스캔 자체의 검증은 아래 수동 검증 참고)

## Task 21: [정합성 검토 R5-05] 관리자 — 환자 계정 병합 후보 관리 화면

3단계(환자 앱) `register_profile`(Task 5)이 전화번호+생년월일+이름 후보가 2건 이상이라 자동 연결하지 못하고 새 행으로 가입시킨 경우, 관리자가 후보를 확인하고 수동으로 병합할 수 있는 화면.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/services/patient_merge_service.py`
- Create: `backend/app/routers/patient_merge.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_patient_merge_service.py`
- Create: `frontend/src/api/patientMerge.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/admin/PatientMergePage.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/App.tsx` (`/admin/patient-merge` 라우트)
- Test: `frontend/src/features/admin/PatientMergePage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.core.security.StaffContext, require_role`(1단계 Task 9), `app.db.admin_client.get_admin_client`(1단계 Task 12)
- Produces: `app.services.patient_merge_service.list_merge_candidates(staff) -> list[dict]`([정합성 검토 R5-05] 전화번호+생년월일+이름이 일치하는 활성 환자를 모두 묶고, 계정 연결된 행이 2건 이상인 그룹만 제외 — 연결/미연결 쌍도 후보에 포함됨), `merge_patients(primary_id: UUID, duplicate_id: UUID, staff) -> None`(`primary_id`에 `auth_user_id`를 없다면 `duplicate_id`의 것으로 연결, `duplicate_id`는 `is_active=false`), `staff_link_family_member(account_patient_id: UUID, target_patient_id: UUID, relation: str, staff) -> None`([정합성 검토 R5-01] 정책 스펙의 `staff_link_family_member` RPC 계약 구현)
- Produces: `GET /admin/patient-merge-candidates`, `POST /admin/patient-merge-candidates/merge` (모두 `require_role("admin")`), `POST /admin/family-links`([정합성 검토 R5-01] `require_role("receptionist", "admin")`)

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`backend/tests/test_patient_merge_service.py`:
```python
import pytest

from app.core.errors import AppError
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.services import patient_merge_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str) -> StaffContext:
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=None)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_merge_candidates_groups_ambiguous_matches(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    for _ in range(2):
        await db_conn.execute(
            "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678')"
        )
    await db_conn.execute(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('이몽룡', '1990-01-01', 'M', '01099998888')"
    )

    groups = await patient_merge_service.list_merge_candidates(admin)

    assert len(groups) == 1
    assert len(groups[0]["patients"]) == 2
    assert groups[0]["patients"][0]["name"] == "홍길동"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_merge_patients_links_auth_and_deactivates_duplicate(db_conn):
    import uuid

    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    auth_user_id = uuid.uuid4()
    await db_conn.execute(
        """
        insert into auth.users (id, email, encrypted_password, email_confirmed_at, created_at, updated_at, aud, role)
        values ($1, $2, '', now(), now(), now(), 'authenticated', 'authenticated')
        """,
        auth_user_id, f"{auth_user_id}@test.local",
    )
    with_auth_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (auth_user_id, name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ($1, '홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id",
        auth_user_id,
    )
    legacy_id = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "insert into patients (name, birth_date, gender, phone) values ('홍길동', '1985-03-01', 'M', '01012345678') returning id"
    )

    await patient_merge_service.merge_patients(primary_id=with_auth_id, duplicate_id=legacy_id, staff=admin)

    duplicate_row = await db_conn.fetchrow("select is_active from patients where id = $1", legacy_id)
    assert duplicate_row["is_active"] is False


@pytest.mark.asyncio

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_merge_patients_succeeds_when_duplicate_holds_auth_user_id(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R5-05] primary_id가 미연결이고 duplicate_id가 연결된 계정인 방향으로 병합해도
    UNIQUE 제약 위반 없이 성공해야 한다(이전 버전은 이 방향에서 즉시 실패했다)."""
    import uuid

    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    auth_user_id = uuid.uuid4()
    await db_conn.execute(

        values ($1, $2, '', now(), now(), now(), 'authenticated', 'authenticated')
        """,

    primary_row = await db_conn.fetchrow("select auth_user_id, is_active from patients where id = $1", legacy_id)
    assert primary_row["auth_user_id"] == auth_user_id

    assert duplicate_row["auth_user_id"] is None
```

`backend/app/services/patient_merge_service.py`:
```python
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.errors import AppError
from app.core.masking import mask_birth_date, mask_phone  # [정합성 검토 R5-06]
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as, get_pool  # get_pool: [정합성 검토 R5-01] staff_link_family_member


async def list_merge_candidates(staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    """[정합성 검토 R5-05] 이름+생년월일+전화번호가 모두 일치하는 환자가 2건 이상인 그룹을 묶어서
    반환한다. 이전 버전은 `auth_user_id is null`인 행끼리만 그룹을 지어, 실제 병합이 필요한 가장
    흔한 경우 — 앱 가입으로 계정이 연결된 행과 전화예약으로 남은 미연결 행의 쌍 — 을 후보 목록에서
    아예 빼먹었다(둘 중 하나는 항상 auth_user_id가 있으므로). 이제는 활성 환자 전체에서 이름·생년월일·
    전화번호가 일치하는 그룹을 모으고, 그중 계정이 연결된 행이 2건 이상인 그룹만(서로 다른 진짜
    사람일 가능성이 높음) 병합 후보에서 제외한다.

    [정합성 검토 R5-06] 이전 버전은 이 목록 응답에 birth_date/phone 원본을 그대로 담아 다른 목록
    (대기목록·취소요청 등)과 다른 기준을 썼다. 다른 목록들과 같은 규칙으로 서버에서 마스킹된
    값만 내려주고, 원본은 상세 확인(감사로그가 남는 화면)에서만 보게 한다."""
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            """
            select id, name, birth_date, phone, auth_user_id
            from patients
            where is_active
              and (name, birth_date, phone) in (
                  select name, birth_date, phone from patients
                  where is_active
                  group by name, birth_date, phone
                  having count(*) >= 2
              )
            order by name, birth_date, phone, id
            """
        )
    groups: dict[tuple, list[dict]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        key = (row["name"], row["birth_date"], row["phone"])
        groups.setdefault(key, []).append(dict(row))
    return [
        {
            "patients": [
                {
                    "id": p["id"], "name": p["name"], "auth_user_id": p["auth_user_id"],
                    "masked_birth_date": mask_birth_date(p["birth_date"]),
                    "masked_phone": mask_phone(p["phone"]),
                }
                for p in patients
            ],
        }
        for patients in groups.values()
        if sum(1 for p in patients if p["auth_user_id"] is not None) <= 1
    ]


async def merge_patients(primary_id: UUID, duplicate_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> None:
    """[정합성 검토 R5-05] primary_id 행에 auth_user_id를 연결하고(둘 중 하나만 있어야 함),
    duplicate_id는 is_active=false로 비활성화한다. R5-01의 '직원 확인 후 연결' 패턴을 재사용한다.

    이전 버전은 duplicate_id 쪽에 있던 auth_user_id를 지우지 않은 채 primary_id에 같은 값을
    설정해, primary_id가 auth_user_id 없이 들어오고 duplicate_id가 실제 연결된 계정인 흔한
    방향(전화예약 환자를 대표로 고르고 앱 계정 쪽을 duplicate로 선택한 경우)에서 `patients.auth_user_id`
    UNIQUE 제약을 즉시 위반했다. 지금은 duplicate 쪽 값을 먼저 비운 뒤 primary에 설정해 순서와
    무관하게 항상 안전하다. `for update`로 두 행을 잠가 동시에 같은 후보를 병합하는 요청이
    겹치지 않게 한다(acquire_as가 이미 전체 블록을 한 트랜잭션으로 감싼다)."""
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        primary = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select auth_user_id from patients where id = $1 for update", primary_id,
        )
        duplicate = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select auth_user_id from patients where id = $1 for update", duplicate_id,
        )
        if primary is None or duplicate is None:
            raise AppError("대상 환자를 찾을 수 없습니다.", status_code=404)
        if primary["auth_user_id"] is not None and duplicate["auth_user_id"] is not None:
            raise AppError("두 행 모두 이미 계정이 연결되어 있어 병합할 수 없습니다.", status_code=409)

        auth_user_id = primary["auth_user_id"] or duplicate["auth_user_id"]
        if auth_user_id is not None:
            # duplicate가 그 값을 들고 있었다면 먼저 비워야 UNIQUE 제약과 충돌하지 않는다.
            await conn.execute("update patients set auth_user_id = null where id = $1", duplicate_id)
            await conn.execute("update patients set auth_user_id = $1 where id = $2", auth_user_id, primary_id)
        await conn.execute(
            "update patients set is_active = false where id = $1", duplicate_id,
        )


async def staff_link_family_member(
    account_patient_id: UUID, target_patient_id: UUID, relation: str, staff: StaffContext,
) -> None:
    """[정합성 검토 R5-01] 정책 스펙(`2026-07-28-p0-policy-decisions-design.md`)이 확정한 세 RPC 계약
    중 `staff_link_family_member`에 해당한다. 나머지 둘은 이미 구현돼 있었지만 이름이 달랐다
    (`create_family_member`→`add_family_member`, `link_existing_family_member`→`confirm_family_link_otp`
    — 3단계 계획, 기능은 동일). 이 함수만 실제로 구현이 빠져 있었다: 전화로 예약한 가족을 접수직원이
    신분증 등으로 직접 본인 확인한 뒤 연결하는 경로. `add_family_member`/`confirm_family_link_otp`와
    같은 이유로(patient_family_links에는 클라이언트 직접 INSERT 정책이 없다) 서비스 역할 커넥션을
    직접 쓰고, 직원이 수행하는 연결이므로 access_audit_log에도 남긴다(R5-08과 연결)."""
    if staff.role not in ("receptionist", "admin"):
        raise AppError("가족 연결은 접수직원만 처리할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)

    pool = await get_pool()
    async with pool.acquire() as conn:
        async with conn.transaction():
            account = await conn.fetchval("select id from patients where id = $1", account_patient_id)
            target = await conn.fetchval("select id from patients where id = $1", target_patient_id)
            if account is None or target is None:
                raise AppError("대상 환자를 찾을 수 없습니다.", status_code=404)

            await conn.execute(
                "insert into patient_family_links (account_patient_id, family_patient_id, relation) "
                "values ($1, $2, $3)",
                account_patient_id, target_patient_id, relation,
            )
            await conn.execute(
                "insert into access_audit_log (staff_id, patient_id, resource_type) values ($1, $2, 'patient_detail')",
                staff.id, target_patient_id,
            )
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 라우터 추가**

`backend/app/routers/patient_merge.py`:
```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.core.security import StaffContext, require_role
from app.services import patient_merge_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin/patient-merge-candidates", tags=["admin"])


@router.get("")
async def list_merge_candidates(staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin"))) -> list[dict]:
    return await patient_merge_service.list_merge_candidates(staff)


class MergeRequest(BaseModel):
    primary_id: UUID
    duplicate_id: UUID


@router.post("/merge")
async def merge_patients(
    body: MergeRequest, staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("admin")),
) -> dict:
    await patient_merge_service.merge_patients(body.primary_id, body.duplicate_id, staff)
    return {"status": "merged"}


# [정합성 검토 R5-01] staff_link_family_member — 별도 prefix로 접수직원도 호출할 수 있게 한다
# (환자 병합은 관리자 전용이지만, 가족 연결 본인확인은 접수 창구에서 접수직원이 처리하는 업무다).
family_link_router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin/family-links", tags=["admin"])


class StaffLinkFamilyMemberRequest(BaseModel):
    account_patient_id: UUID
    target_patient_id: UUID
    relation: str


@family_link_router.post("")
async def staff_link_family_member(
    body: StaffLinkFamilyMemberRequest,
    staff: StaffContext = Depends(require_role("receptionist", "admin")),
) -> dict:
    await patient_merge_service.staff_link_family_member(
        body.account_patient_id, body.target_patient_id, body.relation, staff,
    )
    return {"status": "linked"}
```

`backend/app/main.py`에 `app.include_router(patient_merge.router)`와 `app.include_router(patient_merge.family_link_router)` 추가.

- [ ] **Step 5: frontend API 클라이언트 + 화면**

`frontend/src/api/patientMerge.ts`:
```typescript
import { apiClient } from "./client";

export type MergeGroup = {
  patients: { id: string; name: string; auth_user_id: string | null; masked_birth_date: string; masked_phone: string }[];
};

export async function fetchMergeCandidates(): Promise<MergeGroup[]> {
  const res = await apiClient.get<MergeGroup[]>("/admin/patient-merge-candidates");
  return res.data;
}

export async function mergePatients(primaryId: string, duplicateId: string): Promise<void> {
  await apiClient.post("/admin/patient-merge-candidates/merge", { primary_id: primaryId, duplicate_id: duplicateId });
}
```

`frontend/src/features/admin/PatientMergePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { fetchMergeCandidates, mergePatients } from "../../api/patientMerge";

export function PatientMergePage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: groups = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["merge-candidates"], queryFn: fetchMergeCandidates });

  const handleMerge = async (primaryId: string, duplicateId: string) => {
    await mergePatients(primaryId, duplicateId);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["merge-candidates"] });
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <h1>환자 계정 병합 후보</h1>
      {groups.map((group, i) => (
        <div key={i}>
          {group.patients.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.id}>
              {/* [정합성 검토 R5-06] 목록에는 서버가 이미 마스킹한 값만 표시한다(원본 birth_date/phone 미포함) */}
              {p.name} ({p.masked_birth_date}, {p.masked_phone})
              {group.patients
                .filter((other) => other.id !== p.id)
                .map((other) => (
                  <button key={other.id} onClick={() => handleMerge(p.id, other.id)}>
                    이 행을 대표로, {other.name} 병합
                  </button>
                ))}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## Task 22: [정합성 검토 R5-09] 관리자 — 문진 양식 관리 화면

진료과별 사전문진 양식을 관리자가 만들고 수정하는 화면. `questionnaire_templates.department_id`는 1단계에서 UNIQUE 제약이 걸려(Task 7 Step 6~10) 진료과당 정확히 1행만 존재하므로, 저장은 항상 upsert로 그 1행을 덮어쓴다(초안/승인 2단계 없음, 롤백 없음).

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/services/questionnaire_admin_service.py`
- Create: `backend/app/routers/questionnaire_admin.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_questionnaire_admin_service.py`
- Create: `frontend/src/api/questionnaireAdmin.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/admin/QuestionnaireAdminPage.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/App.tsx` (`/admin/questionnaires` 라우트)
- Test: `frontend/src/features/admin/QuestionnaireAdminPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.core.security.StaffContext, require_role`(1단계 Task 9), `questionnaire_templates.department_id` UNIQUE 제약(1단계 Task 7 Step 6~10)
- Produces: `app.services.questionnaire_admin_service.upsert_template(department_id: UUID, questions: list[dict], staff) -> dict`, `list_templates(staff) -> list[dict]`, `get_template(department_id: UUID, staff) -> dict | None`
- Produces: `GET /admin/questionnaires`, `GET /admin/questionnaires/{department_id}`, `PUT /admin/questionnaires/{department_id}` (모두 `require_role("admin")`)

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`backend/tests/test_questionnaire_admin_service.py`:
```python
import pytest

from app.core.errors import AppError
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.services import questionnaire_admin_service
from tests.conftest import seed_staff


def _to_context(seed: dict, role: str) -> StaffContext:
    return StaffContext(id=seed["staff_id"], auth_user_id=seed["auth_user_id"], role=role, department_id=None)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_upsert_template_creates_then_overwrites(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R5-09] 저장은 즉시 활성화되고, 두 번째 저장은 롤백 없이 그대로 덮어쓴다."""
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('소아과') returning id")

    await questionnaire_admin_service.upsert_template(
        dept_id, [{"text": "old", "type": "text", "required": True}], admin,
    )
    result = await questionnaire_admin_service.upsert_template(
        dept_id, [{"text": "new", "type": "text", "required": True}], admin,
    )

    assert result["questions"][0]["text"] == "new"
    row_count = await db_conn.fetchval(
        "select count(*) from questionnaire_templates where department_id = $1", dept_id,
    )
    assert row_count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_non_admin_cannot_upsert_template(db_conn):
    """[정합성 검토 R5-09] 관리자가 아닌 직원(의사·접수직원)이 저장을 시도하면 거부된다."""
    doctor = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="doctor"), "doctor")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('소아과') returning id")

    with pytest.raises(AppError):
        await questionnaire_admin_service.upsert_template(dept_id, [], doctor)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_list_and_get_template(db_conn):
    admin = _to_context(await seed_staff(db_conn, role="admin"), "admin")
    dept_id = await db_conn.fetchval("insert into departments (name) values ('소아과') returning id")
    await questionnaire_admin_service.upsert_template(dept_id, [{"text": "q1"}], admin)

    templates = await questionnaire_admin_service.list_templates(admin)
    assert len(templates) == 1

    fetched = await questionnaire_admin_service.get_template(dept_id, admin)
    assert fetched["questions"][0]["text"] == "q1"

    empty = await questionnaire_admin_service.get_template(dept_id, admin)  # 두 번 조회해도 안전한지(부수효과 없음)
    assert empty is not None
```

Run: `cd backend && pytest tests/test_questionnaire_admin_service.py -v`
Expected: FAIL(모듈 없음)

- [ ] **Step 2: questionnaire_admin_service 구현**

`backend/app/services/questionnaire_admin_service.py`:
```python
import json
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.errors import AppError
from app.core.security import StaffContext
from app.db.pool import acquire_as


def _require_admin(staff: StaffContext) -> None:
    # [정합성 검토 R5-09] RLS(admin_can_manage_templates)가 최종 방어선이지만, 서비스 계층에서도
    # 이중 검사해 권한 오류를 더 명확한 메시지로 조기에 돌려준다.
    if staff.role != "admin":
        raise AppError("문진 양식 관리는 관리자만 할 수 있습니다.", status_code=403)


async def upsert_template(department_id: UUID, questions: list[dict], staff: StaffContext) -> dict:
    _require_admin(staff)
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            """
            insert into questionnaire_templates (department_id, questions)
            values ($1, $2::jsonb)
            on conflict (department_id) do update set questions = excluded.questions
            returning id, department_id, questions
            """,
            department_id, json.dumps(questions),
        )
    return dict(row)


async def list_templates(staff: StaffContext) -> list[dict]:
    _require_admin(staff)
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        rows = await conn.fetch("select id, department_id, questions from questionnaire_templates order by department_id")
    return [dict(row) for row in rows]


async def get_template(department_id: UUID, staff: StaffContext) -> dict | None:
    _require_admin(staff)
    async with acquire_as(str(staff.auth_user_id)) as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            "select id, department_id, questions from questionnaire_templates where department_id = $1",
            department_id,
        )
    return dict(row) if row else None
```
